- [ ] **Step 7: Rewrite the store with entries and the rebase**

Replace `apps/web/src/features/tree/store.ts` with:

```ts
import {
  applyOp,
  EMPTY_TREE,
  OpError,
  treeStateFromLists,
  type Op,
  type OpErrorCode,
  type TreeState,
} from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { ApiError } from '../../shared/lib/apiError';
import { fetchOpsSince, fetchTree, submitOp } from './api';
import { replayPending, resolveRefs, revertFieldsOf, type PendingEntry } from './pending';
import { rebase } from './rebase';

export type TreeStatus = 'idle' | 'loading' | 'ready' | 'error';
/**
 * What happened to the user's unsent changes. `clanId` is the family tree they belonged to: the
 * notice is shown app-wide and stays until dismissed, whatever tree is on screen.
 */
export type TreeNotice = { clanId: string } &
  (
    /** The whole tree was read again (a third conflict in a row, or an answer it cannot read). */
    | { kind: 'reloaded' }
    /**
     * A change was refused (by the server, or because the rebase found it no longer applies):
     * rolled back, and `dropped` other unsent changes with it.
     */
    | { kind: 'rejected'; error: unknown; dropped: number }
    /** The session expired: sending stopped, every unsent op is kept until the user signs in. */
    | { kind: 'signedOut' }
    /**
     * The server says this tab's cookie is another account's (IDENTITY_MISMATCH): sending
     * stopped, every unsent op is kept for the account that made them.
     */
    | { kind: 'otherAccount' }
    /**
     * `count` ops were still unsent when another tree opened or the account changed: dropped.
     * `inFlight`: the first of them was on its way, so it may have been saved after all.
     */
    | { kind: 'discarded'; count: number; inFlight: boolean }
  );
/** Why apply() did nothing: a broken rule, or no tree to edit (loading or failed to load). */
export type ApplyFailure = OpErrorCode | 'NOT_LOADED';
export type ApplyResult = { ok: true } | { ok: false; reason: ApplyFailure };

/** Waits between retries of a request the server could not take (network, 429, 5xx). */
export const RETRY_DELAYS_MS = [1000, 2000, 4000, 8000, 16000, 30000] as const;
/** The conflict that makes the store read the whole tree again instead of rebasing (§6.3). */
export const MAX_CONFLICTS_IN_A_ROW = 3;

export interface TreeStoreState {
  /** The signed-in account (from the app shell); null until known and after sign-out. */
  userId: string | null;
  clanId: string | null;
  status: TreeStatus;
  loadError: unknown;
  /** What the user sees: the confirmed tree plus every pending entry. */
  tree: TreeState;
  /** What the server has accepted, at confirmedVersion. */
  confirmedTree: TreeState;
  confirmedVersion: number;
  /** Changes not yet accepted, oldest first; pending[0] is the one being sent. */
  pending: PendingEntry[];
  /** True while a failed request waits for its retry. */
  retrying: boolean;
  /**
   * True after a 401 the refresh cookie could not fix: nothing is sent until the next apply(),
   * load() or sign-in of the same account.
   */
  signedOut: boolean;
  /** True after IDENTITY_MISMATCH: nothing is sent until the right account is back. */
  otherAccount: boolean;
  notice: TreeNotice | null;
  load: (clanId: string) => Promise<void>;
  /**
   * The app shell says who is signed in. The same account again resumes a stopped queue;
   * another account drops what the previous one left unsent (spec §7.3).
   */
  setAccount: (userId: string) => void;
  apply: (op: Op) => ApplyResult;
  dismissNotice: () => void;
}

/** For the unsent-changes guard: primitives, so they are stable selectors. */
export const selectHasUnsentChanges = (state: TreeStoreState): boolean => state.pending.length > 0;
export const selectUnsentCount = (state: TreeStoreState): number => state.pending.length;

/** The store's data: every member that is not an action. */
type DataKeys = {
  [K in keyof TreeStoreState]: TreeStoreState[K] extends (...args: never[]) => unknown ? never : K;
}[keyof TreeStoreState];
type TreeData = Pick<TreeStoreState, DataKeys>;

const INITIAL: TreeData = {
  userId: null,
  clanId: null,
  status: 'idle',
  loadError: null,
  tree: EMPTY_TREE,
  confirmedTree: EMPTY_TREE,
  confirmedVersion: 0,
  pending: [],
  retrying: false,
  signedOut: false,
  otherAccount: false,
  notice: null,
};

/** A new session per load(): answers that belong to an older one are ignored. */
let session = 0;
/** A request of the queue (a POST, the rebase's reads, or the reload) is on its way. */
let sending = false;
let retryTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
let attempt = 0;
/** The queue must read the whole tree again (third conflict, or an answer it cannot trust). */
let needsReload = false;
/** The queue must catch up with the log and replay its entries (after a 409). */
let needsRebase = false;
/** 409s since the server last accepted one of this tab's changes. */
let conflictsInARow = 0;
/** The local id of the next pending entry. */
let nextPendingId = 1;
/** The load() in progress, so a second load() of the same tree (StrictMode) shares it. */
let loading: Promise<void> | undefined;

function clearRetry(): void {
  if (retryTimer !== undefined) clearTimeout(retryTimer);
  retryTimer = undefined;
  attempt = 0;
}

function newEntry(op: Op): PendingEntry {
  const id = nextPendingId;
  nextPendingId += 1;
  return { id, op };
}

function isRetryable(error: unknown): boolean {
  return (
    error instanceof ApiError && (error.status === 0 || error.status === 429 || error.status >= 500)
  );
}

function isUnauthenticated(error: unknown): boolean {
  return error instanceof ApiError && error.status === 401;
}

/** The cookie belongs to another account than the one that made the queue. */
function isOtherAccount(error: unknown): boolean {
  return error instanceof ApiError && error.code === 'IDENTITY_MISMATCH';
}

/** A 409 (check IDENTITY_MISMATCH before this one). */
function isConflict(error: unknown): boolean {
  return error instanceof ApiError && error.status === 409;
}

/** A 4xx answer (check 401, 409 and 429 before this one). */
function isRefusal(error: unknown): boolean {
  return error instanceof ApiError && error.status >= 400 && error.status < 500;
}

export const useTreeStore = create<TreeStoreState>()((set, get) => {
  /** Stops the queue after a 401; every pending entry stays. */
  function stopSignedOut(clanId: string): void {
    attempt = 0;
    set({ signedOut: true, retrying: false, notice: { kind: 'signedOut', clanId } });
  }

  /** Stops the queue after IDENTITY_MISMATCH; every pending entry stays for its account. */
  function stopOtherAccount(clanId: string): void {
    attempt = 0;
    set({ otherAccount: true, retrying: false, notice: { kind: 'otherAccount', clanId } });
  }

  /** The user is back (apply, load, or a sign-in after a stop): send again. */
  function resume(): void {
    const { signedOut, otherAccount, notice } = get();
    if (signedOut || otherAccount) {
      const stopNotice = notice?.kind === 'signedOut' || notice?.kind === 'otherAccount';
      set({ signedOut: false, otherAccount: false, notice: stopNotice ? null : notice });
    }
    void flush();
  }

  /** The server refused pending[0]: roll it back, with the entries that depended on it. */
  function reject(clanId: string, error: unknown): void {
    const { confirmedTree, pending } = get();
    const [refused, ...rest] = pending;
    if (refused === undefined) return;
    const replayed = replayPending(confirmedTree, rest, { alreadyDropped: [refused.id] });
    set({
      tree: replayed.tree,
      pending: replayed.kept,
      retrying: false,
      notice: { kind: 'rejected', clanId, error, dropped: replayed.dropped.length },
    });
  }

  function scheduleRetry(mine: number): void {
    const delay = RETRY_DELAYS_MS[Math.min(attempt, RETRY_DELAYS_MS.length - 1)];
    attempt += 1;
    set({ retrying: true });
    retryTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      retryTimer = undefined;
      if (mine === session) void flush();
    }, delay);
  }

  /** A read of the queue (the reload or the rebase) failed: retry, stop, or show the error. */
  function readFailed(clanId: string, error: unknown, mine: number): void {
    if (isUnauthenticated(error)) {
      stopSignedOut(clanId);
    } else if (isRetryable(error)) {
      scheduleRetry(mine);
    } else if (isRefusal(error)) {
      // The tree is gone or no longer ours (404, 403). The pending entries stay in the store:
      // the app-level UnsentChangesGuard still counts them, and the next load() reports them
      // as discarded.
      needsReload = false;
      needsRebase = false;
      attempt = 0;
      set({ status: 'error', loadError: error, retrying: false });
    } else {
      // An answer we cannot read: try again later rather than at once.
      scheduleRetry(mine);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Reads the tree again and drops every pending entry, including entries applied while this
   * request was on its way; the `reloaded` notice tells the user. When the reload fails, the
   * entries are kept and the reload is retried with backoff.
   */
  async function reload(clanId: string, mine: number): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const response = await fetchTree(clanId);
      if (mine !== session) return;
      const tree = treeStateFromLists(response.progenitorId, response.persons, response.unions);
      needsReload = false;
      needsRebase = false;
      conflictsInARow = 0;
      attempt = 0;
      set({
        tree,
        confirmedTree: tree,
        confirmedVersion: response.version,
        pending: [],
        retrying: false,
        notice: { kind: 'reloaded', clanId },
      });
    } catch (error) {
      if (mine !== session) return;
      readFailed(clanId, error, mine);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Spec §6.3: reads what others saved since confirmedVersion and replays the queue on top.
   * Entries that no longer apply are dropped with the `rejected` notice (the first one's reason).
   */
  async function rebaseQueue(clanId: string, userId: string, mine: number): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const remote = await fetchOpsSince(clanId, get().confirmedVersion);
      if (mine !== session) return;
      // Read again after the await: entries applied meanwhile are replayed too.
      const state = get();
      const result = rebase({
        confirmedTree: state.confirmedTree,
        confirmedVersion: state.confirmedVersion,
        pending: state.pending,
        remote,
        userId,
      });
      needsRebase = false;
      attempt = 0;
      if (!result.ok) {
        needsReload = true;
        return;
      }
      if (result.saved) conflictsInARow = 0;
      const [first, ...others] = result.dropped;
      set({
        confirmedTree: result.confirmedTree,
        confirmedVersion: result.confirmedVersion,
        tree: result.tree,
        pending: result.pending,
        retrying: false,
        notice: first
          ? { kind: 'rejected', clanId, error: first.error, dropped: others.length }
          : state.notice,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      if (mine !== session) return;
      readFailed(clanId, error, mine);
    }
  }

  async function send(
    clanId: string,
    userId: string,
    entry: PendingEntry,
    baseVersion: number,
    mine: number,
  ): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const { seq } = await submitOp(clanId, {
        op: entry.op,
        baseVersion,
        expectedUserId: userId,
        ...revertFieldsOf(entry),
      });
      if (mine !== session) return;
      attempt = 0;
      conflictsInARow = 0;
      const state = get();
      set({
        confirmedTree: applyOp(state.confirmedTree, entry.op).tree,
        confirmedVersion: seq,
        pending: resolveRefs(state.pending.slice(1), entry.id, seq),
        retrying: false,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      if (mine !== session) return;
      if (isRetryable(error)) {
        scheduleRetry(mine);
      } else if (isUnauthenticated(error)) {
        stopSignedOut(clanId);
      } else if (isOtherAccount(error)) {
        stopOtherAccount(clanId);
      } else if (isConflict(error)) {
        attempt = 0;
        conflictsInARow += 1;
        // Someone keeps saving first: stop rebasing and read the whole tree.
        if (conflictsInARow >= MAX_CONFLICTS_IN_A_ROW) needsReload = true;
        else needsRebase = true;
      } else if (isRefusal(error)) {
        attempt = 0;
        reject(clanId, error);
      } else {
        // No answer we can trust: a 2xx with an unreadable body (INVALID_RESPONSE; the op may
        // well be saved) or a non-API error. Start again from the server's tree.
        attempt = 0;
        needsReload = true;
      }
    }
  }

  async function flush(): Promise<void> {
    if (sending || retryTimer !== undefined) return;
    const { userId, clanId, status, signedOut, otherAccount, pending, confirmedVersion } = get();
    if (userId === null || clanId === null || status !== 'ready' || signedOut || otherAccount) {
      return;
    }
    const entry = pending[0];
    if (!needsReload && !needsRebase && entry === undefined) return;
    const mine = session;
    sending = true;
    try {
      if (needsReload) await reload(clanId, mine);
      else if (needsRebase) await rebaseQueue(clanId, userId, mine);
      else if (entry !== undefined) await send(clanId, userId, entry, confirmedVersion, mine);
    } finally {
      if (mine === session) sending = false;
    }
    if (mine === session) void flush();
  }

  /** A ready tree with nothing pending opened again: keep it, take a newer version quietly. */
  async function refresh(clanId: string): Promise<void> {
    const mine = session;
    try {
      const response = await fetchTree(clanId);
      const state = get();
      if (mine !== session || sending || needsReload || needsRebase || state.pending.length > 0) {
        return;
      }
      if (response.version <= state.confirmedVersion) return;
      const tree = treeStateFromLists(response.progenitorId, response.persons, response.unions);
      set({ tree, confirmedTree: tree, confirmedVersion: response.version });
    } catch {
      // The tree on screen is still valid; the next op's 409 would catch a missed change.
    }
  }

  return {
    ...INITIAL,

    load(clanId) {
      const current = get();
      if (current.clanId === clanId) {
        if (current.status === 'loading' && loading !== undefined) return loading;
        if (current.status === 'ready') {
          // Coming back while changes are still being saved (or waiting for a sign-in): keep
          // them and send again.
          if (current.pending.length > 0 || needsReload || needsRebase) {
            resume();
            return Promise.resolve();
          }
          return refresh(clanId);
        }
      }

      // A new session. Unsent entries of the previous one are dropped: say how many
      // (UnsentChangesGuard asks before another tree opens and empties the queue itself when
      // the user agrees, so this notice only shows when something else got here). A notice the
      // user has not dismissed yet stays: it may be the only word they get about edits that
      // were dropped while another page was on screen.
      const discarded = current.pending.length;
      const inFlight = sending && !needsReload && !needsRebase;
      const unseen = current.notice;
      session += 1;
      const mine = session;
      sending = false;
      needsReload = false;
      needsRebase = false;
      conflictsInARow = 0;
      clearRetry();
      set({
        ...INITIAL,
        userId: current.userId,
        clanId,
        status: 'loading',
        notice:
          discarded > 0
            ? { kind: 'discarded', clanId: current.clanId ?? clanId, count: discarded, inFlight }
            : unseen,
      });
      const run = (async () => {
        try {
          const response = await fetchTree(clanId);
          if (mine !== session) return;
          const tree = treeStateFromLists(response.progenitorId, response.persons, response.unions);
          set({
            status: 'ready',
            tree,
            confirmedTree: tree,
            confirmedVersion: response.version,
          });
        } catch (error) {
          if (mine === session) set({ status: 'error', loadError: error });
        } finally {
          if (mine === session) loading = undefined;
        }
      })();
      loading = run;
      return run;
    },

    setAccount(userId) {
      const state = get();
      if (state.userId === userId) {
        if (state.signedOut || state.otherAccount) resume();
        return;
      }
      if (state.userId !== null) discardForAccountChange();
      set({ userId });
    },

    apply(op) {
      const state = get();
      // While a reload is pending it would drop every new entry: refuse instead. No account
      // yet: an op could not be tied to the person who made it.
      if (state.status !== 'ready' || needsReload || state.userId === null) {
        return { ok: false, reason: 'NOT_LOADED' };
      }
      try {
        const { tree } = applyOp(state.tree, op);
        set({ tree, pending: [...state.pending, newEntry(op)] });
      } catch (error) {
        if (error instanceof OpError) return { ok: false, reason: error.code };
        throw error;
      }
      resume();
      return { ok: true };
    },

    dismissNotice() {
      set({ notice: null });
    },
  };
});

/** Stops the queue and forgets every answer still on its way. */
function stopSending(): void {
  session += 1;
  sending = false;
  needsReload = false;
  needsRebase = false;
  conflictsInARow = 0;
  loading = undefined;
  clearRetry();
}

/**
 * Forgets the tree and the queue, including the module-level send state: answers still on their
 * way are ignored and nothing more is sent. Keeps the account. For the user discarding unsent
 * changes, a deleted tree, and tests.
 */
export function resetTreeStore(): void {
  const { userId } = useTreeStore.getState();
  stopSending();
  useTreeStore.setState({ ...INITIAL, userId });
}

/** Sign-out: forgets everything, the account too. */
export function signOutTreeStore(): void {
  stopSending();
  useTreeStore.setState(INITIAL);
}

/** Another account signed in: what the previous one left unsent is dropped, with a notice. */
function discardForAccountChange(): void {
  const { pending, clanId } = useTreeStore.getState();
  const inFlight = sending && !needsReload && !needsRebase;
  signOutTreeStore();
  if (pending.length > 0 && clanId !== null) {
    useTreeStore.setState({
      notice: { kind: 'discarded', clanId, count: pending.length, inFlight },
    });
  }
}

/** Forgets the tree store when it holds this family tree (it was deleted); other trees stay. */
export function discardTreeStateFor(clanId: string): void {
  if (useTreeStore.getState().clanId === clanId) resetTreeStore();
}

/** How many changes are not saved yet, for the app shell (sign-out asks first when any). */
export function useUnsentChangeCount(): number {
  return useTreeStore(selectUnsentCount);
}
```

### Task 7: The queue in `sessionStorage`

Unsent entries now survive a reload of the tab and a Google sign-in (a full-page redirect): the store mirrors the queue to `sessionStorage`, keyed by user, clan and confirmed version, and restores it when the tab learns its account (spec §7.3). A queue made at an older version is restored through the log, like a rebase. The login page's Google warning goes: nothing is lost there any more.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/features/tree/persist.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/features/tree/rebase.ts` (`restoreQueue`)
- Modify: `apps/web/src/features/tree/store.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/features/tree/TreeNoticeBanner.tsx`
- Delete: `apps/web/src/features/auth/GoogleSignInWarning.tsx`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/features/auth/LoginPage.tsx`, `apps/web/src/features/auth/RegisterPage.tsx`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/test/setup.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/locales/vi.json`, `apps/web/src/locales/en.json`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `PendingEntry`, `replayPending`, `resolveRefs` (Task 6); `setAccount`, `signOutTreeStore` (Task 3).
- Produces:
  - `features/tree/persist.ts`: `SAVED_QUEUE_KEY = 'ancestors-tree:unsent'`, `SavedQueueSchema`, `type SavedQueue = { format: 1; userId: string; clanId: string; version: number; entries: PendingEntry[] }`, `type SavedQueueRead = { kind: 'none' } | { kind: 'unreadable' } | { kind: 'saved'; queue: SavedQueue }`, `readSavedQueue(): SavedQueueRead`, `writeSavedQueue(queue: SavedQueue): void`, `clearSavedQueue(): void`.
  - `features/tree/rebase.ts`: `RestoreInput`, `RestoreResult`, `restoreQueue(input: RestoreInput): RestoreResult`.
  - Store: `restoredFrom: number | null`; `TreeNotice` gains `{ kind: 'savedUnreadable'; clanId: null }` (so `TreeNotice['clanId']` is `string | null`).

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { CLAN_ID, ME, SON } from './fixtures';
import {
  clearSavedQueue,
  readSavedQueue,
  SAVED_QUEUE_KEY,
  writeSavedQueue,
  type SavedQueue,
} from './persist';

const QUEUE: SavedQueue = {
  format: 1,
  userId: ME.id,
  clanId: CLAN_ID,
  version: 3,
  entries: [
    { id: 1, op: { type: 'updatePerson', payload: { id: SON, changes: { bio: 'Con trưởng' } } } },
    {
      id: 2,
      op: { type: 'updatePerson', payload: { id: SON, changes: { bio: null } } },
      reverts: { pendingId: 1 },
    },
  ],
};

describe('the saved queue', () => {
  it('reads back what was written, and nothing after clearing', () => {
    expect(readSavedQueue()).toEqual({ kind: 'none' });
    writeSavedQueue(QUEUE);
    expect(readSavedQueue()).toEqual({ kind: 'saved', queue: QUEUE });
    clearSavedQueue();
    expect(sessionStorage.getItem(SAVED_QUEUE_KEY)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('is unreadable when broken or of another format', () => {
    sessionStorage.setItem(SAVED_QUEUE_KEY, '{broken');
    expect(readSavedQueue()).toEqual({ kind: 'unreadable' });
    sessionStorage.setItem(SAVED_QUEUE_KEY, JSON.stringify({ ...QUEUE, format: 2 }));
    expect(readSavedQueue()).toEqual({ kind: 'unreadable' });
    sessionStorage.setItem(SAVED_QUEUE_KEY, JSON.stringify({ ...QUEUE, entries: [{ id: 1, op: { type: 'nope' } }] }));
    expect(readSavedQueue()).toEqual({ kind: 'unreadable' });
  });
});
```

```ts
describe('restoreQueue', () => {
  /** The server's tree now: version 4, with Bình's child added by someone else. */
  const now = () => {
    const result = rebase(input([]));
    if (!result.ok) throw new Error('expected a rebase');
    return result.confirmedTree;
  };
  const restoring = (pending: PendingEntry[], remote = [remoteEntry(4, looseChild)]) => ({
    tree: now(),
    version: 4,
    savedVersion: 3,
    pending,
    remote,
    userId: ME.id,
  });

  it('replays the saved entries on the tree as it is now', () => {
    const result = restoreQueue(restoring([{ id: 1, op: bio('Con trưởng') }]));
    if (!result.ok) throw new Error('expected a restore');
    expect(result.tree.persons[CHILD]).toBeDefined();
    expect(result.tree.persons[SON]?.bio).toBe('Con trưởng');
    expect(result.pending).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('confirms a saved entry that reached the server before the reload', () => {
    const remote = [remoteEntry(4, bio('Con trưởng'), ME.id)];
    const tree = rebase(input([], remote));
    if (!tree.ok) throw new Error('expected a rebase');
    const result = restoreQueue({ ...restoring([{ id: 1, op: bio('Con trưởng') }], remote), tree: tree.confirmedTree });
    if (!result.ok) throw new Error('expected a restore');
    expect(result.saved?.seq).toBe(4);
    expect(result.pending).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('ignores log entries after the tree it was given, and refuses a gap', () => {
    const later = [remoteEntry(4, looseChild), remoteEntry(5, bio('Sau'))];
    expect(restoreQueue(restoring([], later)).ok).toBe(true);
    expect(restoreQueue(restoring([], [])).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(restoreQueue({ ...restoring([]), savedVersion: 5 }).ok).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

```ts
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `persist.ts` and `restoreQueue`**

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/persist.ts`:

```ts
import { IdSchema } from '@ancestors-tree/shared';
import { OpSchema } from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import { z } from 'zod';

/** One queue per tab: sessionStorage outlives a reload and a sign-in redirect, not the tab. */
export const SAVED_QUEUE_KEY = 'ancestors-tree:unsent';

const OpRefSchema = z.union([
  z.object({ seq: z.number().int().min(1) }),
  z.object({ pendingId: z.number().int().min(1) }),
]);

const SavedEntrySchema = z.object({
  id: z.number().int().min(1),
  op: OpSchema,
  reverts: OpRefSchema.optional(),
  revertsToSeq: z.number().int().min(1).optional(),
});

/** The unsent entries of one account and family tree, made on `version` (spec §7.3). */
export const SavedQueueSchema = z.object({
  format: z.literal(1),
  userId: IdSchema,
  clanId: IdSchema,
  version: z.number().int().min(0),
  entries: z.array(SavedEntrySchema).min(1),
});

export type SavedQueue = z.infer<typeof SavedQueueSchema>;
export type SavedQueueRead =
  | { kind: 'none' }
  | { kind: 'unreadable' }
  | { kind: 'saved'; queue: SavedQueue };

export function readSavedQueue(): SavedQueueRead {
  let text: string | null;
  try {
    text = sessionStorage.getItem(SAVED_QUEUE_KEY);
  } catch {
    // Storage is disabled: there is nothing to restore.
    return { kind: 'none' };
  }
  if (text === null) return { kind: 'none' };
  try {
    const parsed = SavedQueueSchema.safeParse(JSON.parse(text));
    return parsed.success ? { kind: 'saved', queue: parsed.data } : { kind: 'unreadable' };
  } catch {
    return { kind: 'unreadable' };
  }
}

export function writeSavedQueue(queue: SavedQueue): void {
  try {
    sessionStorage.setItem(SAVED_QUEUE_KEY, JSON.stringify(queue));
  } catch {
    // Storage is full or disabled: the queue still lives in memory, as in P5.
  }
}

export function clearSavedQueue(): void {
  try {
    sessionStorage.removeItem(SAVED_QUEUE_KEY);
  } catch {
    // Storage is disabled: nothing was saved.
  }
}
```

Add to `apps/web/src/features/tree/rebase.ts`:

```ts
export interface RestoreInput {
  /** The server's tree now, at `version`. */
  tree: TreeState;
  version: number;
  /** The confirmed version the saved entries were made on. */
  savedVersion: number;
  pending: readonly PendingEntry[];
  /** The log after savedVersion; entries past `version` are ignored. */
  remote: readonly OperationEntry[];
  userId: string;
}

export type RestoreResult =
  | { ok: false }
  | {
      ok: true;
      saved: SavedEntry | null;
      tree: TreeState;
      pending: PendingEntry[];
      dropped: DroppedEntry[];
      inverses: Map<number, Op>;
    };

/**
 * A queue saved before a reload meets the server's tree as it is now. Like rebase(), it finds
 * the first entry already saved by this account and replays the rest; `ok: false` when the log
 * between the two versions has a gap.
 */
export function restoreQueue(input: RestoreInput): RestoreResult {
  const missed = input.remote.filter((remote) => remote.seq <= input.version);
  const contiguous =
    input.version >= input.savedVersion &&
    missed.length === input.version - input.savedVersion &&
    missed.every((remote, index) => remote.seq === input.savedVersion + index + 1);
  if (!contiguous) return { ok: false };
  const head = input.pending[0];
  let saved: SavedEntry | null = null;
  let othersSaved = false;
  for (const remote of missed) {
    if (saved === null && head && remote.userId === input.userId && sameOp(remote.op, head.op)) {
      saved = { entry: head, seq: remote.seq };
    } else {
      othersSaved = true;
    }
  }
  const rest = saved ? resolveRefs(input.pending.slice(1), saved.entry.id, saved.seq) : [...input.pending];
  const replayed = replayPending(input.tree, rest, { othersSaved });
  return {
    ok: true,
    saved,
    tree: replayed.tree,
    pending: replayed.kept,
    dropped: replayed.dropped,
    inverses: replayed.inverses,
  };
}
```

Inside the store creator, after `refresh`, add:

```ts
  /**
   * load() of a tree whose queue came from sessionStorage: reads the tree, the log since the
   * saved version when the tree moved on, and replays the queue (restoreQueue). A failure keeps
   * the queue and `restoredFrom`, so the next load() tries again.
   */
  function restoreSession(clanId: string): Promise<void> {
    session += 1;
    const mine = session;
    sending = false;
    needsReload = false;
    needsRebase = false;
    conflictsInARow = 0;
    clearRetry();
    set({ status: 'loading', loadError: null });
    const run = (async () => {
      try {
        const response = await fetchTree(clanId);
        if (mine !== session) return;
        const savedVersion = get().restoredFrom ?? response.version;
        const remote =
          response.version > savedVersion ? await fetchOpsSince(clanId, savedVersion) : [];
        if (mine !== session) return;
        const state = get();
        const fetched = treeStateFromLists(response.progenitorId, response.persons, response.unions);
        const base = {
          status: 'ready' as const,
          confirmedTree: fetched,
          confirmedVersion: response.version,
          restoredFrom: null,
        };
        const result = restoreQueue({
          tree: fetched,
          version: response.version,
          savedVersion,
          pending: state.pending,
          remote,
          userId: state.userId ?? '',
        });
        if (!result.ok) {
          // The saved queue does not fit the log: start from the server's tree and say so.
          set({ ...base, tree: fetched, pending: [], notice: { kind: 'reloaded', clanId } });
          return;
        }
        const [first, ...others] = result.dropped;
        set({
          ...base,
          tree: result.tree,
          pending: result.pending,
          notice: first
            ? { kind: 'rejected', clanId, error: first.error, dropped: others.length }
            : state.notice,
        });
        void flush();
      } catch (error) {
        if (mine === session) set({ status: 'error', loadError: error });
      } finally {
        if (mine === session) loading = undefined;
      }
    })();
    loading = run;
    return run;
  }

  /**
   * The first account this tab learns (after a reload or a sign-in redirect) picks up the queue
   * saved before: restored for the same account (then loaded and sent, whatever page is open),
   * dropped with a notice when another account made it or another tree is already open.
   */
  function restoreSaved(userId: string, saved: SavedQueueRead): void {
    if (saved.kind === 'none') return;
    if (saved.kind === 'unreadable') {
      set({ notice: { kind: 'savedUnreadable', clanId: null } });
      return;
    }
    const { queue } = saved;
    const current = get();
    if (queue.userId !== userId || (current.clanId !== null && current.clanId !== queue.clanId)) {
      set({
        notice: { kind: 'discarded', clanId: queue.clanId, count: queue.entries.length, inFlight: false },
      });
      return;
    }
    // A load of the same tree may be on its way: the restore replaces it.
    stopSending();
    nextPendingId = Math.max(nextPendingId, ...queue.entries.map((entry) => entry.id + 1));
    set({
      ...INITIAL,
      userId,
      clanId: queue.clanId,
      pending: queue.entries,
      confirmedVersion: queue.version,
      restoredFrom: queue.version,
      notice: current.notice,
    });
    void get().load(queue.clanId);
  }
```

```ts
// Mirrors the queue to sessionStorage, so a reload or a Google sign-in keeps it (spec §7.3).
useTreeStore.subscribe((state, previous) => {
  if (
    state.pending === previous.pending &&
    state.confirmedVersion === previous.confirmedVersion &&
    state.userId === previous.userId &&
    state.clanId === previous.clanId
  ) {
    return;
  }
  // No account yet (a reload before the app knows who is signed in): keep what is saved.
  if (state.userId === null) return;
  if (state.clanId === null || state.pending.length === 0) {
    clearSavedQueue();
    return;
  }
  writeSavedQueue({
    format: 1,
    userId: state.userId,
    clanId: state.clanId,
    version: state.confirmedVersion,
    entries: state.pending,
  });
});
```

### Task 8: Undo and redo in the store; `describeOp`

Spec §6.4: undo applies the locally computed inverse and queues it as a new op with `revertsSeq`; redo applies the change again. Both stacks hold this user's changes in this session only, and follow every queue change: a saved entry is named by its seq, a dropped entry leaves both stacks, a kept entry gets the inverse it has on the rebased tree. `describeOp` turns an op into words, for the undo badge (Task 9) and the history (Task 11).

- Modify: `apps/web/src/features/tree/pending.ts` (stack types and helpers)
- Modify: `apps/web/src/features/tree/store.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/features/tree/describeOp.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/locales/vi.json`, `apps/web/src/locales/en.json` (`tree.change.*`)
- Test: `apps/web/src/features/tree/pending.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/features/tree/store.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/features/tree/describeOp.test.ts` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `PendingEntry`, `OpRef`, `replayPending`, `resolveRefs` (Task 6); the store of Task 7.
- Produces:
  - `features/tree/pending.ts`: `interface UndoEntry { op: Op; inverse: Op; ref: OpRef }`, `interface RedoEntry { op: Op; ref: OpRef }`, `MAX_UNDO_STEPS = 100`, `pushCapped<T>(stack: readonly T[], item: T): T[]`, `resolveStack<T extends { ref: OpRef }>(stack: readonly T[], pendingId: number, seq: number): T[]`, `withoutRefsTo<T extends { ref: OpRef }>(stack: readonly T[], pendingIds: Iterable<number>): T[]`, `withoutPendingRefs<T extends { ref: OpRef }>(stack: readonly T[]): T[]`, `withInverses(stack: readonly UndoEntry[], inverses: ReadonlyMap<number, Op>): UndoEntry[]`.

  - `features/tree/describeOp.ts`:
    - `interface NameLookup { person(id: string): string | undefined; union(id: string): readonly string[] | undefined }`
    - `lookupIn(tree: TreeState): NameLookup`, `lookupWith(lookup: NameLookup, ops: readonly Op[]): NameLookup`

```ts
import { treeStateFromLists, type Op } from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import i18n from '../../shared/i18n';
import { describeAction, describeOp, lookupIn, lookupWith } from './describeOp';
import { FAMILY, fixturePerson, GRANDMA, GRANDPA, LOOSE, PARENTS_UNION, SON } from './fixtures';

const NEW_ID = '64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f6a0f1';
const NEW_UNION = '64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f6b0f1';
const lookup = lookupIn(treeStateFromLists(FAMILY.progenitorId, FAMILY.persons, FAMILY.unions));
const say = (op: Op) => describeAction(i18n.t, describeOp(op, lookup));

describe('describeOp', () => {
  it('names the people a change is about', () => {
    expect(say({ type: 'addPerson', payload: fixturePerson(NEW_ID, 'Nguyễn Văn Út') })).toBe(
      'thêm Nguyễn Văn Út',
    );
    expect(say({ type: 'updatePerson', payload: { id: SON, changes: { bio: 'x' } } })).toBe(
      'sửa thông tin của Nguyễn Văn Nam',
    );
    expect(say({ type: 'deletePerson', payload: { id: LOOSE } })).toBe('xoá Trần Văn Bình');
    expect(say({ type: 'setProgenitor', payload: { personId: GRANDPA } })).toBe(
      'chọn Nguyễn Văn Tư làm thuỷ tổ',
    );
    expect(say({ type: 'setProgenitor', payload: { personId: null } })).toBe('bỏ chọn thuỷ tổ');
    expect(say({ type: 'setParents', payload: { personId: LOOSE, parentUnionId: PARENTS_UNION } })).toBe(
      'đặt Trần Văn Bình làm con của Nguyễn Văn Tư và Lê Thị Hoa',
    );
    expect(say({ type: 'setParents', payload: { personId: SON, parentUnionId: null } })).toBe(
      'gỡ Nguyễn Văn Nam khỏi cha mẹ',
    );
    expect(say({ type: 'deleteUnion', payload: { id: PARENTS_UNION } })).toBe(
      'xoá hôn nhân của Nguyễn Văn Tư và Lê Thị Hoa',
    );
  });

  it('describes a batch by the one person it adds, or counts', () => {
    const spouse: Op = {
      type: 'batch',
      payload: {
        ops: [
          { type: 'addPerson', payload: fixturePerson(NEW_ID, 'Phạm Thị Lụa', { gender: 'female' }) },
          { type: 'addUnion', payload: { id: NEW_UNION, partnerIds: [SON, NEW_ID], order: 1, status: 'married' } },
        ],
      },
    };
    expect(say(spouse)).toBe('thêm Phạm Thị Lụa');
    const several: Op = {
      type: 'batch',
      payload: {
        ops: [
          { type: 'addPerson', payload: fixturePerson(NEW_ID, 'A') },
          { type: 'addPerson', payload: fixturePerson(NEW_UNION, 'B') },
        ],
      },
    };
    expect(say(several)).toBe('thêm 2 người');
    const mixed: Op = {
      type: 'batch',
      payload: {
        ops: [
          { type: 'updatePerson', payload: { id: SON, changes: { bio: 'x' } } },
          { type: 'setProgenitor', payload: { personId: GRANDMA } },
        ],
      },
    };
    expect(say(mixed)).toBe('2 thay đổi');
  });

  it('finds names in the ops themselves, and says "someone" when there is none', () => {
    const gone = fixturePerson(NEW_ID, 'Đỗ Văn Khoa');
    const restore: Op = { type: 'restorePerson', payload: { person: gone, unions: [] } };
    const remove: Op = { type: 'deletePerson', payload: { id: NEW_ID } };
    expect(describeAction(i18n.t, describeOp(remove, lookupWith(lookup, [restore])))).toBe(
      'xoá Đỗ Văn Khoa',
    );
    expect(say(remove)).toBe('xoá một người');
  });
});
```

```ts
describe('undo and redo', () => {
  it('undoes and redoes a change, also one not saved yet', async () => {
    const held = gate();
    const bodies: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = [];
    mockApi({
      ...treeRoutes(),
      [OPS]: async ({ body }) => {
        bodies.push(body as Record<string, unknown>);

    // Redo is the change made again: no revertsSeq.

    expect(store().undoStack.map((entry) => entry.ref)).toEqual([{ seq: 6 }]);

    expect(store().undo()).toBeNull();
    expect(store().redo()).toBeNull();

        },

    expect(store().undo()).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: 'PROGENITOR_PROTECTED' });
    expect(store().undoStack).toEqual([]);

    await store().load(CLAN_ID);
    store().apply(bio('Một'));

    expect(store().redoStack).toEqual([]);

    await store().load(CLAN_ID);

      ...treeRoutes(),
      [OPS_LOG]: () => ({ body: [remoteEntry(4, bio('Từ máy khác'))] }),
      [OPS]: () => {
        posts += 1;

    await store().load(CLAN_ID);
    store().apply(bio('Của tôi'));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(store().pending).toHaveLength(0));
    expect(store().undoStack).toEqual([
      { op: bio('Của tôi'), inverse: bio('Từ máy khác'), ref: { seq: 5 } },
    ]);
  });

  it(`keeps the latest ${MAX_UNDO_STEPS} steps`, async () => {
    mockApi({ ...treeRoutes(), [OPS]: () => new Promise(() => undefined) });
    await store().load(CLAN_ID);
    for (let index = 0; index <= MAX_UNDO_STEPS; index += 1) store().apply(bio(`Lần ${index}`));
    expect(store().undoStack).toHaveLength(MAX_UNDO_STEPS);
    expect(store().undoStack[0]?.op).toEqual(bio('Lần 1'));
  });
});
```

Add to `apps/web/src/features/tree/pending.ts`:

```ts
/** One step of the undo stack: the change made, the op that undoes it, and which change it is. */
export interface UndoEntry {
  op: Op;
  inverse: Op;
  ref: OpRef;
}

/** One step of the redo stack: the change to make again, and the undo it came from. */
export interface RedoEntry {
  op: Op;
  ref: OpRef;
}

/** The undo and redo stacks keep this many steps (the oldest go first). */
export const MAX_UNDO_STEPS = 100;

export function pushCapped<T>(stack: readonly T[], item: T): T[] {
  const next = [...stack, item];
  return next.length > MAX_UNDO_STEPS ? next.slice(next.length - MAX_UNDO_STEPS) : next;
}

function isPendingRef(ref: OpRef, ids: ReadonlySet<number>): boolean {
  return 'pendingId' in ref && ids.has(ref.pendingId);
}

/** Pending entry `pendingId` was saved as `seq`. */
export function resolveStack<T extends { ref: OpRef }>(
  stack: readonly T[],
  pendingId: number,
  seq: number,
): T[] {
  const ids = new Set([pendingId]);
  return stack.map((step) => (isPendingRef(step.ref, ids) ? { ...step, ref: { seq } } : step));
}

/** These pending entries were dropped: their steps go too. */
export function withoutRefsTo<T extends { ref: OpRef }>(
  stack: readonly T[],
  pendingIds: Iterable<number>,
): T[] {
  const ids = new Set(pendingIds);
  return stack.filter((step) => !isPendingRef(step.ref, ids));
}

/** The whole tree was read again: steps of unsent changes go, saved ones stay. */
export function withoutPendingRefs<T extends { ref: OpRef }>(stack: readonly T[]): T[] {
  return stack.filter((step) => 'seq' in step.ref);
}

/** The queue was replayed on another tree: kept entries undo with their new inverse. */
export function withInverses(
  stack: readonly UndoEntry[],
  inverses: ReadonlyMap<number, Op>,
): UndoEntry[] {
  return stack.map((step) => {
    const inverse = 'pendingId' in step.ref ? inverses.get(step.ref.pendingId) : undefined;
    return inverse === undefined ? step : { ...step, inverse };
  });
}
```

Inside the store creator, before `stopSignedOut`, add:

```ts
  type Applied = { ok: true; entry: PendingEntry } | { ok: false; reason: ApplyFailure };

  /**
   * Applies a change on screen and queues it. `stacks` gives the new undo and redo stacks for
   * the entry and its inverse, so the tree, the queue and the stacks change in one update.
   */
  function applyChange(
    op: Op,
    extra: Pick<PendingEntry, 'reverts' | 'revertsToSeq'>,
    stacks: (entry: PendingEntry, inverse: Op, state: TreeStoreState) => Partial<TreeData>,
  ): Applied {
    const state = get();
    // While a reload is pending it would drop every new entry: refuse instead. No account yet:
    // an op could not be tied to the person who made it.
    if (state.status !== 'ready' || needsReload || state.userId === null) {
      return { ok: false, reason: 'NOT_LOADED' };
    }
    let result: ApplyOpResult;
    try {
      result = applyOp(state.tree, op);
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof OpError) return { ok: false, reason: error.code };
      throw error;
    }
    const entry: PendingEntry = { ...newEntry(op), ...extra };
    set({
      tree: result.tree,
      pending: [...state.pending, entry],
      ...stacks(entry, result.inverse, state),
    });
    resume();
    return { ok: true, entry };
  }

  function toApplyResult(applied: Applied): ApplyResult {
    return applied.ok ? { ok: true } : applied;
  }
```

```ts
    apply(op) {
      return toApplyResult(
        applyChange(op, {}, (entry, inverse, state) => ({
          undoStack: pushCapped(state.undoStack, { op, inverse, ref: { pendingId: entry.id } }),
          redoStack: [],
        })),
      );
    },

    undo() {
      const step = get().undoStack.at(-1);
      if (step === undefined) return null;
      const applied = applyChange(step.inverse, { reverts: step.ref }, (entry, _inverse, state) => ({
        undoStack: state.undoStack.slice(0, -1),
        // Redo makes the undone change again (not the undo's inverse): the history reads as
        // the same change.
        redoStack: pushCapped(state.redoStack, { op: step.op, ref: { pendingId: entry.id } }),
      }));
      // The inverse no longer applies (someone changed the tree): the step cannot come back.
      if (!applied.ok && applied.reason !== 'NOT_LOADED') {
        set({ undoStack: get().undoStack.slice(0, -1) });
      }
      return toApplyResult(applied);
    },

    redo() {
      const step = get().redoStack.at(-1);
      if (step === undefined) return null;
      const applied = applyChange(step.op, {}, (entry, inverse, state) => ({
        redoStack: state.redoStack.slice(0, -1),
        undoStack: pushCapped(state.undoStack, {
          op: step.op,
          inverse,
          ref: { pendingId: entry.id },
        }),
      }));
      if (!applied.ok && applied.reason !== 'NOT_LOADED') {
        set({ redoStack: get().redoStack.slice(0, -1) });

      return toApplyResult(applied);
```

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/describeOp.ts`:

```ts
import type { Op, SingleOp, TreeState } from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import type { TFunction } from 'i18next';

/** Names of people and the partners of unions, wherever they can be found. */
export interface NameLookup {
  person(id: string): string | undefined;
  union(id: string): readonly string[] | undefined;
}

export function lookupIn(tree: TreeState): NameLookup {
  return {
    person: (id) => (Object.hasOwn(tree.persons, id) ? tree.persons[id]?.fullName : undefined),
    union: (id) => (Object.hasOwn(tree.unions, id) ? tree.unions[id]?.partnerIds : undefined),
  };
}

/** `lookup`, falling back to the people and unions the ops carry (someone deleted since). */
export function lookupWith(lookup: NameLookup, ops: readonly Op[]): NameLookup {
  const persons = new Map<string, string>();
  const unions = new Map<string, readonly string[]>();
  const collect = (op: Op): void => {
    switch (op.type) {
      case 'batch':
        op.payload.ops.forEach(collect);
        break;
      case 'addPerson':
        persons.set(op.payload.id, op.payload.fullName);
        break;
      case 'restorePerson':
        persons.set(op.payload.person.id, op.payload.person.fullName);
        for (const union of op.payload.unions) unions.set(union.id, union.partnerIds);
        break;
      case 'addUnion':
      case 'restoreUnion':
        unions.set(op.payload.id, op.payload.partnerIds);
        break;
      default:
        break;
    }
  };
  ops.forEach(collect);
  return {
    person: (id) => lookup.person(id) ?? persons.get(id),
    union: (id) => lookup.union(id) ?? unions.get(id),
  };
}

/** What a change did, with the names it is about (null: nobody knows the name any more). */
export type OpDescription =
  | { kind: 'addPerson' | 'updatePerson' | 'deletePerson' | 'restorePerson'; name: string | null }
  | { kind: 'addPeople' | 'changes'; count: number }
  | { kind: 'addUnion' | 'updateUnion' | 'deleteUnion' | 'restoreUnion'; names: (string | null)[] }
  | { kind: 'setParents'; name: string | null; parents: (string | null)[] }
  | { kind: 'detachParents'; name: string | null }
  | { kind: 'setProgenitor'; name: string | null }
  | { kind: 'clearProgenitor' };

function describeSingle(op: SingleOp, names: NameLookup): OpDescription {
  const person = (id: string) => names.person(id) ?? null;
  const partners = (id: string, fallback: readonly string[] = []) =>
    (names.union(id) ?? fallback).map(person);
  switch (op.type) {
    case 'addPerson':
      return { kind: 'addPerson', name: op.payload.fullName };
    case 'updatePerson':
      return { kind: 'updatePerson', name: person(op.payload.id) };
    case 'deletePerson':
      return { kind: 'deletePerson', name: person(op.payload.id) };
    case 'restorePerson':
      return { kind: 'restorePerson', name: op.payload.person.fullName };
    case 'addUnion':
    case 'restoreUnion':
      return { kind: op.type, names: op.payload.partnerIds.map(person) };
    case 'updateUnion':
      return { kind: 'updateUnion', names: partners(op.payload.id, op.payload.changes.partnerIds) };
    case 'deleteUnion':
      return { kind: 'deleteUnion', names: partners(op.payload.id) };
    case 'setParents':
      return op.payload.parentUnionId === null
        ? { kind: 'detachParents', name: person(op.payload.personId) }
        : {
            kind: 'setParents',
            name: person(op.payload.personId),
            parents: partners(op.payload.parentUnionId),
          };
    case 'setProgenitor':
      return op.payload.personId === null
        ? { kind: 'clearProgenitor' }
        : { kind: 'setProgenitor', name: person(op.payload.personId) };
  }
}

const PERSON_OPS = new Set<SingleOp['type']>(['addPerson', 'deletePerson', 'restorePerson']);

/**
 * One change in words. A batch reads as the one person it adds, deletes or restores (adding a
 * child or a spouse also adds a union), as "add N people", or else as "N changes".
 */
export function describeOp(op: Op, lookup: NameLookup): OpDescription {
  const names = lookupWith(lookup, [op]);
  if (op.type !== 'batch') return describeSingle(op, names);
  const ops = op.payload.ops;
  const people = ops.filter((child) => PERSON_OPS.has(child.type));
  const [onlyPerson] = people;
  if (people.length === 1 && onlyPerson) return describeSingle(onlyPerson, names);
  if (people.length > 1 && ops.every((child) => child.type === 'addPerson' || child.type === 'addUnion')) {
    return { kind: 'addPeople', count: people.length };
  }
  const [only] = ops;
  if (ops.length === 1 && only) return describeSingle(only, names);
  return { kind: 'changes', count: ops.length };
}

function nameOf(t: TFunction, name: string | null): string {
  return name ?? t('tree.change.someone');
}

function namesOf(t: TFunction, names: readonly (string | null)[]): string {
  const [first, second] = names;
  if (names.length >= 2) {
    return t('tree.change.pair', { first: nameOf(t, first ?? null), second: nameOf(t, second ?? null) });
  }
  return nameOf(t, first ?? null);
}

/** The change as a lower-case phrase: "thêm Nguyễn Văn Út", "xoá hôn nhân của A và B". */
export function describeAction(t: TFunction, description: OpDescription): string {
  switch (description.kind) {
    case 'addPerson':
    case 'updatePerson':
    case 'deletePerson':
    case 'restorePerson':
    case 'detachParents':
    case 'setProgenitor':
      return t(`tree.change.${description.kind}`, { name: nameOf(t, description.name) });
    case 'addPeople':
    case 'changes':
      return t(`tree.change.${description.kind}`, { count: description.count });
    case 'addUnion':
    case 'updateUnion':
    case 'deleteUnion':
    case 'restoreUnion':
      return t(`tree.change.${description.kind}`, { names: namesOf(t, description.names) });
    case 'setParents':
      return t('tree.change.setParents', {
        name: nameOf(t, description.name),
        parents: namesOf(t, description.parents),
      });
    case 'clearProgenitor':
      return t('tree.change.clearProgenitor');
  }
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { isTypingTarget, shortcutAction, shortcutLabel } from './shortcuts';

const key = (k: string, mods: Partial<Record<'ctrlKey' | 'metaKey' | 'shiftKey' | 'altKey', boolean>> = {}) => ({
  key: k,
  ctrlKey: false,
  metaKey: false,
  shiftKey: false,
  altKey: false,
  ...mods,
});

describe('shortcutAction', () => {
  it('reads Ctrl/⌘+Z as undo, Ctrl/⌘+Shift+Z and Ctrl+Y as redo', () => {
    expect(shortcutAction(key('z', { ctrlKey: true }))).toBe('undo');
    expect(shortcutAction(key('z', { metaKey: true }))).toBe('undo');
    expect(shortcutAction(key('Z', { ctrlKey: true, shiftKey: true }))).toBe('redo');
    expect(shortcutAction(key('z', { metaKey: true, shiftKey: true }))).toBe('redo');
    expect(shortcutAction(key('y', { ctrlKey: true }))).toBe('redo');
  });

  it('ignores other keys and modifiers', () => {
    expect(shortcutAction(key('z'))).toBeNull();
    expect(shortcutAction(key('z', { ctrlKey: true, altKey: true }))).toBeNull();
    expect(shortcutAction(key('y', { metaKey: true }))).toBeNull();
    expect(shortcutAction(key('x', { ctrlKey: true }))).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('isTypingTarget', () => {
  it('is true in form fields and dialogs, false elsewhere', () => {
    document.body.innerHTML = `
      <input id="name" /><textarea id="bio"></textarea><div contenteditable="true" id="note"></div>
      <div role="dialog"><button id="inDialog">x</button></div><button id="plain">y</button>`;
    for (const id of ['name', 'bio', 'note', 'inDialog']) {
      expect(isTypingTarget(document.getElementById(id)), id).toBe(true);
    }
    expect(isTypingTarget(document.getElementById('plain'))).toBe(false);
    expect(isTypingTarget(null)).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('shortcutLabel', () => {
  it('shows the keys of the platform', () => {
    expect(shortcutLabel('undo', false)).toBe('Ctrl+Z');
    expect(shortcutLabel('redo', false)).toBe('Ctrl+Y');
    expect(shortcutLabel('undo', true)).toBe('⌘Z');
    expect(shortcutLabel('redo', true)).toBe('⇧⌘Z');
  });
});
```

```tsx
import type { Op } from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import { act, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { mockApi } from '../../test/api';
import { mockReactFlow } from '../../test/reactFlow';
import { renderRoute } from '../../test/render';
import { CLAN, CLAN_ID, FAMILY, GRANDPA, ME, SON, treeRoutes } from './fixtures';
import { resetTreeStore, signOutTreeStore, useTreeStore } from './store';

const EDITOR = { ...CLAN, role: 'editor' as const };
const bio: Op = { type: 'updatePerson', payload: { id: SON, changes: { bio: 'Con trưởng' } } };

function editable(clan = EDITOR) {
  let seq = FAMILY.version;
  mockApi({
    ...treeRoutes(FAMILY, clan),
    [`POST /clans/${CLAN_ID}/ops`]: () => {
      seq += 1;
      return { body: { seq } };
    },
  });
}

async function openTree() {
  renderRoute(`/clans/${CLAN_ID}/tree`);
  await screen.findByRole('figure', { name: 'Nguyễn Văn Nam, đời thứ 2' });
}

beforeEach(() => {
  mockReactFlow();
  signOutTreeStore();
  useTreeStore.getState().setAccount(ME.id);
});

afterEach(() => {
  resetTreeStore();
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
});

describe('the undo and redo buttons', () => {
  it('are disabled with nothing to undo, and say the shortcut', async () => {
    editable();
    await openTree();
    const toolbar = screen.getByRole('group', { name: 'Công cụ của cây' });
    const undo = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Hoàn tác' });
    expect(toolbar).toContainElement(undo);
    expect(undo).toBeDisabled();
    expect(undo).toHaveAccessibleDescription('Hoàn tác (Ctrl+Z)');
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Làm lại' })).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByText('Đã lưu')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('name the next undo, undo it, and redo it', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    editable();
    await openTree();
    act(() => void useTreeStore.getState().apply(bio));
    expect(screen.getByText('Hoàn tác: sửa thông tin của Nguyễn Văn Nam')).toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Hoàn tác' }));
    expect(useTreeStore.getState().tree.persons[SON]?.bio).toBeUndefined();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Hoàn tác' })).toBeDisabled();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Làm lại' }));
    expect(useTreeStore.getState().tree.persons[SON]?.bio).toBe('Con trưởng');
    await waitFor(() => expect(useTreeStore.getState().pending).toHaveLength(0));
  });

  it('answer the keyboard, but not while typing', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    editable();
    await openTree();
    act(() => void useTreeStore.getState().apply(bio));
    await user.keyboard('{Control>}z{/Control}');
    expect(useTreeStore.getState().tree.persons[SON]?.bio).toBeUndefined();
    await user.keyboard('{Control>}{Shift>}z{/Shift}{/Control}');
    expect(useTreeStore.getState().tree.persons[SON]?.bio).toBe('Con trưởng');

    const field = document.createElement('input');
    document.body.append(field);
    field.focus();
    await user.keyboard('{Control>}z{/Control}');
    expect(useTreeStore.getState().tree.persons[SON]?.bio).toBe('Con trưởng');
    field.remove();
  });

  it('say why an undo no longer applies', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    editable();
    await openTree();
    act(() =>
      useTreeStore.setState({
        undoStack: [{ op: bio, inverse: { type: 'deletePerson', payload: { id: GRANDPA } }, ref: { seq: 3 } }],
      }),
    );
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Hoàn tác' }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent(
      'Không hoàn tác được: Không xoá được thuỷ tổ. Hãy chọn thuỷ tổ khác trước.',
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Hoàn tác' })).toBeDisabled();
  });

  it('are not shown to a viewer, who still sees the save status', async () => {
    editable({ ...CLAN, role: 'viewer' });
    await openTree();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Hoàn tác' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Đã lưu')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the shortcuts**

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/shortcuts.ts`:

```ts
export type ShortcutAction = 'undo' | 'redo';

type KeyInfo = Pick<KeyboardEvent, 'key' | 'ctrlKey' | 'metaKey' | 'shiftKey' | 'altKey'>;

/** Spec §6.4: Ctrl/⌘+Z undoes; Ctrl/⌘+Shift+Z and Ctrl+Y redo. */
export function shortcutAction(event: KeyInfo): ShortcutAction | null {
  if (event.altKey || !(event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey)) return null;
  const key = event.key.toLowerCase();
  if (key === 'z') return event.shiftKey ? 'redo' : 'undo';
  if (key === 'y' && event.ctrlKey && !event.shiftKey) return 'redo';
  return null;
}

/**
 * Shortcuts are ignored while typing (the field's own undo wins) and while a dialog is open
 * (the change would happen behind it).
 */
export function isTypingTarget(target: EventTarget | null): boolean {
  if (!(target instanceof Element)) return false;
  return (
    target.closest(
      'input, textarea, select, [contenteditable=""], [contenteditable="true"], [role="dialog"], [role="alertdialog"]',
    ) !== null
  );
}

export function isMacPlatform(): boolean {
  return /Mac|iPhone|iPad/.test(navigator.userAgent);
}

/** The keys shown in the tooltip. */
export function shortcutLabel(action: ShortcutAction, mac: boolean): string {
  if (mac) return action === 'undo' ? '⌘Z' : '⇧⌘Z';
  return action === 'undo' ? 'Ctrl+Z' : 'Ctrl+Y';
}

/** For aria-keyshortcuts. */
export const KEY_SHORTCUTS: Record<ShortcutAction, string> = {
  undo: 'Control+Z Meta+Z',
  redo: 'Control+Shift+Z Meta+Shift+Z Control+Y',
};
```

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/UndoRedoButtons.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Redo2, Undo2, type LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useId, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { Button } from '../../shared/ui';
import { describeAction, describeOp, lookupIn, lookupWith } from './describeOp';
import { applyFailureMessage } from './format';
import {
  isMacPlatform,
  isTypingTarget,
  KEY_SHORTCUTS,
  shortcutAction,
  shortcutLabel,
  type ShortcutAction,
} from './shortcuts';
import { useTreeStore } from './store';

interface ToolbarButtonProps {
  label: string;
  tooltip: string;
  icon: LucideIcon;
  keyShortcuts: string;
  disabled: boolean;
  onClick: () => void;
}

/** An icon button whose tooltip (also its description) shows on hover and keyboard focus. */
function ToolbarButton({ label, tooltip, icon: Icon, keyShortcuts, disabled, onClick }: ToolbarButtonProps) {
  const tooltipId = useId();
  return (
    <span className="group relative inline-flex">
      <Button
        variant="ghost"
        size="icon"
        aria-label={label}
        aria-describedby={tooltipId}
        aria-keyshortcuts={keyShortcuts}
        disabled={disabled}
        onClick={onClick}
      >
        <Icon aria-hidden="true" strokeWidth={1.75} className="size-5" />
      </Button>
      <span
        role="tooltip"
        id={tooltipId}
        className="pointer-events-none absolute top-full left-1/2 z-10 mt-1 hidden -translate-x-1/2 rounded-control bg-ink px-2 py-1 text-xs whitespace-nowrap text-paper group-focus-within:block group-hover:block"
      >
        {tooltip}
      </span>
    </span>
  );
}

/** ↶ and ↷ with the next undo named (spec §6.4). Editors only. */
export function UndoRedoButtons() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  // Objects already in the store: stable selector results.
  const nextUndo = useTreeStore((state) => state.undoStack.at(-1) ?? null);
  const nextRedo = useTreeStore((state) => state.redoStack.at(-1) ?? null);
  const tree = useTreeStore((state) => state.tree);
  const undo = useTreeStore((state) => state.undo);
  const redo = useTreeStore((state) => state.redo);
  const [failure, setFailure] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const mac = useMemo(isMacPlatform, []);

  const run = useCallback(
    (action: ShortcutAction) => {
      const result = action === 'undo' ? undo() : redo();
      if (result === null) return;
      setFailure(
        result.ok
          ? null
          : t(action === 'undo' ? 'tree.undo.failed' : 'tree.redo.failed', {
              reason: applyFailureMessage(t, result.reason),
            }),
      );
    },
    [redo, t, undo],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const action = shortcutAction(event);
      if (action === null || isTypingTarget(event.target)) return;
      event.preventDefault();
      run(action);
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, [run]);

  const nextAction = nextUndo
    ? describeAction(t, describeOp(nextUndo.op, lookupWith(lookupIn(tree), [nextUndo.inverse])))
    : null;

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
      <ToolbarButton
        label={t('tree.undo.label')}
        tooltip={t('tree.undo.tooltip', { shortcut: shortcutLabel('undo', mac) })}
        icon={Undo2}
        keyShortcuts={KEY_SHORTCUTS.undo}
        disabled={nextUndo === null}
        onClick={() => run('undo')}
      />
      <ToolbarButton
        label={t('tree.redo.label')}
        tooltip={t('tree.redo.tooltip', { shortcut: shortcutLabel('redo', mac) })}
        icon={Redo2}
        keyShortcuts={KEY_SHORTCUTS.redo}
        disabled={nextRedo === null}
        onClick={() => run('redo')}
      />
      {nextAction !== null ? (
        <span className="rounded-control bg-paper-grid px-2 py-1 text-sm text-ink-muted">
          {t('tree.undo.next', { action: nextAction })}
        </span>
      ) : null}
      {failure !== null ? (
        <p role="alert" className="text-sm font-medium text-stamp">
          {failure}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Task 10: Board keyboard pan and zoom, and cards brought into view

Roadmap P6 item: keyboard pan and zoom for the board, and a focused off-screen card brought into view. The board becomes one tab stop (arrow keys pan, `+`/`=` zoom in, `-` zooms out, `0` shows the whole tree), three zoom buttons follow the cards, and a card whose button gets focus is centred when it is off screen. A newly added person is brought into view at the current zoom instead of zooming out to the whole tree. Arrow keys on a card's own button do nothing yet: spec §7.8 reserves them for moving between relatives, which comes with the profile panel (deviation recorded at the top).

- Create: `apps/web/src/features/tree/boardView.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/features/tree/TreeBoard.tsx`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/locales/vi.json`, `apps/web/src/locales/en.json` (`tree.board.zoomIn`, `zoomOut`, `fitView`, `keyboardHint`)
- Test: `apps/web/src/features/tree/boardView.test.ts` (new), `apps/web/src/features/tree/TreeBoard.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `TreeLayout`, `CARD` (`layout.ts`); `useReducedMotion`, `Button` (`shared/ui`).
- Produces (`features/tree/boardView.ts`):
  - `PAN_STEP = 80`, `REVEAL_MARGIN = 16`;
  - `interface Rect { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number }`, `interface Size { width: number; height: number }`;
  - `panned(viewport: Viewport, key: string): Viewport | null`;
  - `isRectVisible(rect: Rect, viewport: Viewport, size: Size, margin?: number): boolean`;

  - `firstNewPerson(previous: ReadonlySet<string>, layout: TreeLayout): string | null`.

In `apps/web/src/features/tree/TreeBoard.test.tsx`, change `import { screen } from '@testing-library/react';` to `import { screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';` and add this helper after `showTree`:

```ts
/** React Flow writes the viewport as `translate(Xpx, Ypx) scale(Z)` on its viewport element. */
function viewportOf(board: HTMLElement): { x: number; y: number; zoom: number } {
  const element = board.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.react-flow__viewport');
  const match = element?.style.transform.match(
    /translate\((-?[\d.]+)px, ?(-?[\d.]+)px\) scale\(([\d.]+)\)/,
  );
  if (!match) throw new Error(`no viewport transform: ${element?.style.transform ?? 'none'}`);
  return { x: Number(match[1]), y: Number(match[2]), zoom: Number(match[3]) };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the view helpers**

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/boardView.ts`:

```ts
import type { Viewport } from '@xyflow/react';
import { CARD, type TreeLayout } from './layout';

/** Screen pixels moved by one arrow key. */
export const PAN_STEP = 80;
/** A card closer than this to the board's edge counts as off screen. */
export const REVEAL_MARGIN = 16;

export interface Rect {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

export interface Size {
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

/** The arrow keys move the view the way the arrow points: ArrowLeft shows what is to the left. */
export function panned(viewport: Viewport, key: string): Viewport | null {
  switch (key) {
    case 'ArrowLeft':
      return { ...viewport, x: viewport.x + PAN_STEP };
    case 'ArrowRight':
      return { ...viewport, x: viewport.x - PAN_STEP };
    case 'ArrowUp':
      return { ...viewport, y: viewport.y + PAN_STEP };
    case 'ArrowDown':
      return { ...viewport, y: viewport.y - PAN_STEP };
    default:
      return null;
  }
}

/** True when the whole rectangle (board coordinates) is on screen, `margin` pixels from the edge. */
export function isRectVisible(
  rect: Rect,
  viewport: Viewport,
  size: Size,
  margin = REVEAL_MARGIN,
): boolean {
  const left = rect.x * viewport.zoom + viewport.x;
  const top = rect.y * viewport.zoom + viewport.y;
  const right = left + rect.width * viewport.zoom;
  const bottom = top + rect.height * viewport.zoom;
  return (
    left >= margin && top >= margin && right <= size.width - margin && bottom <= size.height - margin
  );
}

export function personRect(layout: TreeLayout, personId: string): Rect | null {
  const person = layout.persons.find((placed) => placed.id === personId);
  return person ? { x: person.x, y: person.y, width: CARD.width, height: CARD.height } : null;
}

/** The first person on the board (in layout order) who was not on it before. */
export function firstNewPerson(previous: ReadonlySet<string>, layout: TreeLayout): string | null {
  return layout.persons.find((placed) => !previous.has(placed.id))?.id ?? null;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Rewrite the board**

Replace `apps/web/src/features/tree/TreeBoard.tsx` with:

```tsx
import { ReactFlow, ReactFlowProvider, useReactFlow } from '@xyflow/react';
import '@xyflow/react/dist/base.css';
import { Maximize, ZoomIn, ZoomOut, type LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react';
import {
  useCallback,
  useEffect,
  useId,
  useMemo,
  useRef,
  type FocusEvent,
  type KeyboardEvent,
  type RefObject,
} from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { Button, useReducedMotion } from '../../shared/ui';
import { firstNewPerson, isRectVisible, panned, personRect } from './boardView';
import { toFlowElements, type FlowElements } from './flowElements';
import { layoutTree, type TreeLayout } from './layout';
import { PersonNode } from './PersonNode';
import { useTreeStore } from './store';
import { ThreadEdge } from './ThreadEdge';
import { UnionNode } from './UnionNode';

// Module constants: new objects on every render would make React Flow remount every node.
const nodeTypes = { person: PersonNode, union: UnionNode };
const edgeTypes = { thread: ThreadEdge };
const FIT_VIEW_OPTIONS = { maxZoom: 1, padding: 0.1 };

/** Centres a card at the current zoom when it is not fully on screen. */
function useReveal(layout: TreeLayout, surface: RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>) {
  const { getViewport, setCenter } = useReactFlow();
  const reducedMotion = useReducedMotion();
  return useCallback(
    (personId: string) => {
      const rect = personRect(layout, personId);
      const element = surface.current;
      if (rect === null || element === null) return;
      const viewport = getViewport();
      const size = { width: element.offsetWidth, height: element.offsetHeight };
      if (isRectVisible(rect, viewport, size)) return;
      void setCenter(rect.x + rect.width / 2, rect.y + rect.height / 2, {
        zoom: viewport.zoom,
        duration: reducedMotion ? 0 : 300,
      });
    },
    [getViewport, layout, reducedMotion, setCenter, surface],
  );
}

/** Brings the first newly added person into view; removals leave the view alone. */
function RevealNewPeople({
  layout,
  reveal,
}: {
  layout: TreeLayout;
  reveal: (personId: string) => void;
}) {
  const seen = useRef<ReadonlySet<string> | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const previous = seen.current;
    seen.current = new Set(layout.persons.map((placed) => placed.id));
    if (previous === null) return; // The first render fits the whole tree instead.
    const added = firstNewPerson(previous, layout);
    if (added === null) return;
    const frame = requestAnimationFrame(() => reveal(added));
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
  }, [layout, reveal]);
  return null;
}

function ZoomButton({
  label,
  icon: Icon,
  onClick,
}: {
  label: string;
  icon: LucideIcon;
  onClick: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <Button variant="secondary" size="icon" aria-label={label} title={label} onClick={onClick}>
      <Icon aria-hidden="true" strokeWidth={1.75} className="size-5" />
    </Button>
  );
}

interface BoardSurfaceProps {
  label: string;
  layout: TreeLayout;
  elements: FlowElements;
}

function BoardSurface({ label, layout, elements }: BoardSurfaceProps) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const { getViewport, setViewport, zoomIn, zoomOut, fitView } = useReactFlow();
  const reducedMotion = useReducedMotion();
  const surface = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const reveal = useReveal(layout, surface);
  const hintId = useId();
  const duration = reducedMotion ? 0 : 200;

  const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    // Keys on a card's own button belong to the card (spec §7.8 reserves the arrows there).
    if (event.target !== event.currentTarget) return;
    if (event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey || event.altKey) return;
    const next = panned(getViewport(), event.key);
    if (next !== null) void setViewport(next, { duration });
    else if (event.key === '+' || event.key === '=') void zoomIn({ duration });
    else if (event.key === '-') void zoomOut({ duration });
    else if (event.key === '0') void fitView({ ...FIT_VIEW_OPTIONS, duration });
    else return;
    event.preventDefault();
  };

  const onFocus = (event: FocusEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    const personId = event.target.closest('.react-flow__node')?.getAttribute('data-id');
    if (personId) reveal(personId);
  };

  return (
    <div
      ref={surface}
      role="group"
      aria-label={label}
      aria-describedby={hintId}
      tabIndex={0}
      onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
      onFocus={onFocus}
      className="board-grid relative h-[70vh] min-h-[420px] overflow-hidden rounded-control border border-line"
    >
      <p id={hintId} className="sr-only">
        {t('tree.board.keyboardHint')}
      </p>
      <ReactFlow
        nodes={elements.nodes}
        edges={elements.edges}
        nodeTypes={nodeTypes}
        edgeTypes={edgeTypes}
        nodesDraggable={false}
        nodesConnectable={false}
        elementsSelectable={false}
        // Nodes and edges are decoration around the cards' own controls: no tab stops on them.
        nodesFocusable={false}
        edgesFocusable={false}
        fitView
        fitViewOptions={FIT_VIEW_OPTIONS}
        minZoom={0.2}
        maxZoom={2}
      >
        <RevealNewPeople layout={layout} reveal={reveal} />
      </ReactFlow>
      {/* After the cards in the DOM, so the tab order is board, cards, zoom buttons. */}
      <div className="absolute right-3 bottom-3 z-10 flex flex-col gap-2">
        <ZoomButton
          label={t('tree.board.zoomIn')}
          icon={ZoomIn}
          onClick={() => void zoomIn({ duration })}
        />
        <ZoomButton
          label={t('tree.board.zoomOut')}
          icon={ZoomOut}
          onClick={() => void zoomOut({ duration })}
        />
        <ZoomButton
          label={t('tree.board.fitView')}
          icon={Maximize}
          onClick={() => void fitView({ ...FIT_VIEW_OPTIONS, duration })}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function TreeBoard({ label }: { label: string }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const tree = useTreeStore((state) => state.tree);
  const layout = useMemo(() => layoutTree(tree), [tree]);
  const previous = useRef<FlowElements | undefined>(undefined);
  const elements = useMemo(() => toFlowElements(layout, previous.current), [layout]);
  useEffect(() => {
    previous.current = elements;
  }, [elements]);

  if (layout.persons.length === 0) {
    return <p className="text-ink-muted">{t('tree.board.noProgenitor')}</p>;
  }
  return (
    <ReactFlowProvider>
      <BoardSurface label={label} layout={layout} elements={elements} />
    </ReactFlowProvider>
  );
}
```

### Task 11: History page with "Revert" on one change

Spec §6.4: every member sees the history, newest first, with the author, the time and a summary; editors and up can revert one change (its inverse, sent as a new op with `revertsSeq`). An editor gets no "Revert" on a change whose inverse needs the Owner (choosing the progenitor), since the server would refuse it. The page loads the tree into the store (the revert is applied there, so it is optimistic, queued, rebased and undoable like any edit), so the unsent-changes guard now also asks before another tree's history page opens.

- Create: `apps/web/src/features/tree/useClanTree.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/features/tree/treePaths.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/features/history/api.ts`

- Create: `apps/web/src/features/history/HistoryEntryItem.tsx`

- Create: `apps/web/src/features/history/fixtures.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/features/history/index.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/features/tree/store.ts` (`revertChange`)
- Modify: `apps/web/src/features/tree/TreePage.tsx` (uses `useClanTree`)
- Modify: `apps/web/src/features/tree/UnsentChangesGuard.tsx` (uses `treeOpenedBy` from `treePaths.ts`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `HistoryEntry`, `HistoryPage`, `HistoryPageSchema`, `HISTORY_PAGE_SIZE` (Task 5, from `@ancestors-tree/shared`); `applyChange`, `toApplyResult`, `pushCapped` (Task 8); `describeOp`, `describeAction`, `lookupIn`, `lookupWith` (Task 8); `SaveStatus`; `applyFailureMessage`; `needsOwner` (`@ancestors-tree/shared/tree`).

  - `features/tree/treePaths.ts`: `treeOpenedBy(pathname: string): string | null` (`/clans/:id`, `/clans/:id/tree`, `/clans/:id/history`).
  - `features/tree/index.ts` also exports `SaveStatus`, `useClanTree`, `applyFailureMessage`, `describeAction`, `describeOp`, `lookupIn`, `lookupWith`, `type NameLookup`.
  - `features/history/api.ts`: `historyKeys.list(clanId) = ['clans', clanId, 'history']`, `fetchHistoryPage(clanId: string, before: number | null, signal?: AbortSignal, limit?: number): Promise<HistoryPage>`, `useHistory(clanId: string)` (an infinite query; pages are `HistoryPage`).

```ts
import type { HistoryEntry } from '@ancestors-tree/shared';
import type { Op } from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import type { MockRoutes } from '../../test/api';
import { CLAN_ID, fixturePerson, GRANDPA, ME, SON } from '../tree/fixtures';

/** Another member of the family tree, the author of the newest change. */
export const AUTHOR_ID = '64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f60720';

export function historyEntry(
  seq: number,
  op: Op,
  inverse: Op,
  overrides: Partial<HistoryEntry> = {},
): HistoryEntry {
  return {
    seq,
    userId: ME.id,
    authorName: ME.name,
    createdAt: `2026-11-1${seq}T08:00:00.000Z`,
    op,
    inverse,
    ...overrides,
  };
}

/** The changes behind FAMILY's version 3, newest first (only the three the tests need). */
export const FAMILY_HISTORY: HistoryEntry[] = [
  historyEntry(
    3,
    { type: 'updatePerson', payload: { id: SON, changes: { fullName: 'Nguyễn Văn Nam' } } },
    { type: 'updatePerson', payload: { id: SON, changes: { fullName: 'Nguyễn Văn Năm' } } },
    { userId: AUTHOR_ID, authorName: 'Trần Minh' },
  ),
  historyEntry(
    2,
    { type: 'setProgenitor', payload: { personId: GRANDPA } },
    { type: 'setProgenitor', payload: { personId: null } },
  ),
  historyEntry(
    1,
    { type: 'addPerson', payload: fixturePerson(GRANDPA, 'Nguyễn Văn Tư') },
    { type: 'deletePerson', payload: { id: GRANDPA } },
  ),
];

/** One page of history, newest first. */
export function historyRoutes(entries: HistoryEntry[] = FAMILY_HISTORY): MockRoutes {
  return {
    [`GET /clans/${CLAN_ID}/history`]: () => ({ body: { entries, nextBefore: null } }),
  };
}
```

```ts
describe('history text', () => {
```
