```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { mockApi, type ApiCall } from '../../test/api';
import { renderRoute } from '../../test/render';
import { resetTreeStore, signOutTreeStore, useTreeStore } from '../tree';
import { CLAN, CLAN_ID, FAMILY, ME, SON, treeRoutes } from '../tree/fixtures';
import { FAMILY_HISTORY, historyEntry, historyRoutes } from './fixtures';

type Role = 'owner' | 'editor' | 'viewer';

function historyApp(role: Role, routes = historyRoutes()) {
  let seq = FAMILY.version;
  const posted: ApiCall[] = [];
  const api = mockApi({
    ...treeRoutes(FAMILY, { ...CLAN, role }),
    ...routes,
    [`POST /clans/${CLAN_ID}/ops`]: (call) => {
      posted.push(call);
      seq += 1;
      return { body: { seq } };
    },
  });
  renderRoute(`/clans/${CLAN_ID}/history`);
  return { api, posted };
}

async function entries() {
  const list = await screen.findByRole('list', { name: 'Lịch sử thay đổi' });
  return within(list).getAllByRole('listitem');
}

beforeEach(() => {
  signOutTreeStore();
  useTreeStore.getState().setAccount(ME.id);
});

afterEach(() => {
  resetTreeStore();
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
});

describe('HistoryPage', () => {
  it('lists the changes newest first with the author, the time and a summary', async () => {
    historyApp('viewer');
    const items = await entries();
    expect(items.map((item) => item.querySelector('p')?.textContent)).toEqual([
      'Trần Minh: sửa thông tin của Nguyễn Văn Nam',
      'Lan: chọn Nguyễn Văn Tư làm thuỷ tổ',
      'Lan: thêm Nguyễn Văn Tư',
    ]);
    expect(within(items[0]!).getByText('#3')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(items[0]!.querySelector('time')).toHaveAttribute('datetime', '2026-11-13T08:00:00.000Z');
    // Viewers read the history; they cannot revert.
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: /Hoàn tác/ })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('is reached from the family tree tabs', async () => {
    historyApp('viewer');
    const tabs = within(await screen.findByRole('navigation', { name: 'Mục của cây gia phả' }));
    expect(tabs.getByRole('link', { name: 'Lịch sử' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-current', 'page');
  });

  it('lets an editor revert one change, sent with the change it reverts', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const { posted } = historyApp('editor');
    await entries();
    // The progenitor is the Owner's to change: no revert of #2 for an editor.
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Hoàn tác thay đổi #2' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Hoàn tác thay đổi #3' }));
    expect(useTreeStore.getState().tree.persons[SON]?.fullName).toBe('Nguyễn Văn Năm');
    await waitFor(() => expect(posted).toHaveLength(1));
    expect(posted[0]!.body).toEqual({
      op: FAMILY_HISTORY[0]!.inverse,
      baseVersion: FAMILY.version,
      expectedUserId: ME.id,
      revertsSeq: 3,
    });
    // One undo step, like any other change.
    expect(useTreeStore.getState().undoStack).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('shows the Owner every revert, and says why one no longer applies', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    historyApp('owner');
    await entries();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Hoàn tác thay đổi #2' })).toBeEnabled();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Hoàn tác thay đổi #1' }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent(
      'Không hoàn tác được thay đổi #1: Không xoá được thuỷ tổ. Hãy chọn thuỷ tổ khác trước.',
    );
  });

  it('refreshes after a change is saved', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const { api } = historyApp('editor');
    await entries();
    const reads = () => api.calls.filter((call) => call.path.endsWith('/history')).length;
    expect(reads()).toBe(1);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Hoàn tác thay đổi #3' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(reads()).toBe(2));
  });

  it('loads older pages', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const [newest, ...older] = FAMILY_HISTORY;
    historyApp('viewer', {
      [`GET /clans/${CLAN_ID}/history`]: ({ url }) =>
        url.searchParams.get('before') === '3'
          ? { body: { entries: older, nextBefore: null } }
          : { body: { entries: [newest], nextBefore: 3 } },
    });
    expect(await entries()).toHaveLength(1);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Xem thêm' }));
    await waitFor(async () => expect(await entries()).toHaveLength(3));
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Xem thêm' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('says when nothing has changed yet', async () => {
    historyApp('viewer', historyRoutes([]));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Chưa có thay đổi nào.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('marks a revert in the list', async () => {
    const op = { type: 'setProgenitor', payload: { personId: null } } as const;
    historyApp('viewer', historyRoutes([historyEntry(4, op, op, { revertsSeq: 2 })]));
    const [item] = await entries();
    expect(item).toHaveTextContent('Hoàn tác thay đổi #2');
  });
});
```

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/useClanTree.ts`:

```ts
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useTreeStore } from './store';

export type ClanTreeView =
  | { state: 'loading' }
  | { state: 'error'; error: unknown; retry: () => void }
  | { state: 'ready' };

/** Loads a family tree into the store for a page that shows or changes it. */
export function useClanTree(clanId: string): ClanTreeView {
  const load = useTreeStore((state) => state.load);
  const loadedClanId = useTreeStore((state) => state.clanId);
  const status = useTreeStore((state) => state.status);
  const loadError = useTreeStore((state) => state.loadError);

  // Also when the store was reset while the page stayed open (another account signed in): the
  // page would otherwise wait for good.
  const needsLoad = loadedClanId !== clanId && status === 'idle';
  useEffect(() => {
    void load(clanId);
  }, [clanId, load, needsLoad]);

  if (loadedClanId === clanId && status === 'error') {
    return { state: 'error', error: loadError, retry: () => void load(clanId) };
  }
  if (loadedClanId !== clanId || status !== 'ready') return { state: 'loading' };
  return { state: 'ready' };
}
```

Replace `apps/web/src/features/tree/TreePage.tsx` with (the same page, loading through the hook):

```tsx
import { hasRoleAtLeast, type Clan } from '@ancestors-tree/shared';
import { useCallback } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { useOutletContext } from 'react-router';
import { errorMessageKey } from '../../shared/lib/apiError';
import { Button } from '../../shared/ui';
import { EmptyTree } from './EmptyTree';
import { useTreeStore } from './store';
import { TreeActionsContext } from './treeActions';
import { TreeBoard } from './TreeBoard';
import { TreeDialogs, useTreeDialog } from './TreeDialogs';
import { TreeSkeleton } from './TreeSkeleton';
import { TreeToolbar } from './TreeToolbar';
import { UnlinkedTray } from './UnlinkedTray';
import { useClanTree } from './useClanTree';

export function TreePage() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const clan = useOutletContext<Clan>();
  const view = useClanTree(clan.id);
  const isEmpty = useTreeStore((state) => Object.keys(state.tree.persons).length === 0);
  const { dialog, changeDialog, firstPersonAdded, returnFocus } = useTreeDialog();
  // Stable, so the cards (context consumers) do not re-render when the page does.
  const openActions = useCallback(
    (personId: string) => changeDialog({ kind: 'actions', personId }),
    [changeDialog],
  );
  const canEdit = hasRoleAtLeast(clan.role, 'editor');

  if (view.state === 'error') {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-start gap-3">
        <p role="alert" className="font-medium text-stamp">
          {t(errorMessageKey(view.error))}
        </p>
        <Button variant="secondary" onClick={view.retry}>
          {t('tree.retry')}
        </Button>
      </div>
    );
  }
  if (view.state === 'loading') return <TreeSkeleton />;
  return (
    <TreeActionsContext value={canEdit ? openActions : null}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <TreeToolbar canEdit={canEdit} />
        {isEmpty ? (
          <EmptyTree onAddFirst={canEdit ? () => changeDialog({ kind: 'first' }) : undefined} />
        ) : (
          <>
            <TreeBoard label={t('tree.board.label', { name: clan.name })} />
            <UnlinkedTray />
          </>
        )}
      </div>
      {canEdit ? (
        <TreeDialogs
          dialog={dialog}
          isOwner={clan.role === 'owner'}
          onDialogChange={changeDialog}
          onFirstPersonAdded={firstPersonAdded}
          onCloseAutoFocus={returnFocus}
        />
      ) : null}
    </TreeActionsContext>
  );
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/features/tree/treePaths.ts`:

```ts
import { matchPath } from 'react-router';

/** Pages that load a family tree into the store; `/clans/:id` redirects to the tree. */
const TREE_PAGES = ['/clans/:id', '/clans/:id/tree', '/clans/:id/history'];

/** The family tree a path loads, or null. */
export function treeOpenedBy(pathname: string): string | null {
  for (const pattern of TREE_PAGES) {
    const id = matchPath(pattern, pathname)?.params.id;
    if (id !== undefined) return id;
  }
  return null;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the history feature**

Create `apps/web/src/features/history/api.ts`:

```ts
import { HistoryPageSchema, type HistoryPage } from '@ancestors-tree/shared';
import { useInfiniteQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { apiRequest } from '../../shared/lib/apiClient';

export const historyKeys = {
  list: (clanId: string) => ['clans', clanId, 'history'] as const,
};

/**
 * One page of changes, newest first, older than `before` (the newest page when null). `limit`
 * defaults to the server's page size.
 */
export function fetchHistoryPage(
  clanId: string,
  before: number | null,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
  limit?: number,
): Promise<HistoryPage> {
  const params = new URLSearchParams();
  if (before !== null) params.set('before', String(before));
  if (limit !== undefined) params.set('limit', String(limit));
  const query = params.toString();
  return apiRequest(`/clans/${clanId}/history${query ? `?${query}` : ''}`, HistoryPageSchema, {
    signal,
  });
}

export function useHistory(clanId: string) {
  return useInfiniteQuery({
    queryKey: historyKeys.list(clanId),
    queryFn: ({ pageParam, signal }) => fetchHistoryPage(clanId, pageParam, signal),
    initialPageParam: null as number | null,
    getNextPageParam: (last) => last.nextBefore,
  });
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/features/history/historyText.ts`:

```ts
import type { HistoryEntry } from '@ancestors-tree/shared';
import type { TFunction } from 'i18next';
import { describeAction, describeOp, lookupWith, type NameLookup } from '../tree';

/** "Lan: thêm Nguyễn Văn Bình". Names come from the tree, else from the ops themselves. */
export function entrySummary(t: TFunction, entry: HistoryEntry, lookup: NameLookup): string {
  const names = lookupWith(lookup, [entry.op, entry.inverse]);
  const action = describeAction(t, describeOp(entry.op, names));
  return t('history.entry.summary', { author: entry.authorName, action });
}

/** Which change a revert undid, or null for an ordinary change. */
export function entryNote(t: TFunction, entry: HistoryEntry): string | null {
  if (entry.revertsSeq !== undefined) return t('history.entry.reverts', { seq: entry.revertsSeq });
  if (entry.revertsToSeq !== undefined) {
    return t('history.entry.revertsTo', { seq: entry.revertsToSeq });
  }
  return null;
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/features/history/HistoryEntryItem.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { HistoryEntry } from '@ancestors-tree/shared';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import type { NameLookup } from '../tree';
import { entryNote, entrySummary } from './historyText';

interface HistoryEntryItemProps {
  entry: HistoryEntry;
  lookup: NameLookup;
  /** The entry's buttons. */
  children?: ReactNode;
}

export function HistoryEntryItem({ entry, lookup, children }: HistoryEntryItemProps) {
  const { t, i18n } = useTranslation();
  const note = entryNote(t, entry);
  const time = new Intl.DateTimeFormat(i18n.language, {
    dateStyle: 'short',
    timeStyle: 'short',
  }).format(new Date(entry.createdAt));
  return (
    <li className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3 border-b border-line py-3">
      <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-col gap-1">
        <p className="text-ink">{entrySummary(t, entry, lookup)}</p>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-3 text-sm text-ink-muted">
          <span className="font-mono">{t('history.entry.number', { seq: entry.seq })}</span>
          <time dateTime={entry.createdAt}>{time}</time>
          {note !== null ? <span>{note}</span> : null}
        </div>
      </div>
      {children ? <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">{children}</div> : null}
    </li>
  );
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/features/history/HistoryPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { hasRoleAtLeast, type Clan, type HistoryEntry } from '@ancestors-tree/shared';
import { needsOwner } from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useEffect, useId, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { useOutletContext } from 'react-router';
import { errorMessageKey } from '../../shared/lib/apiError';
import { Button } from '../../shared/ui';
import { applyFailureMessage, lookupIn, SaveStatus, useClanTree, useTreeStore } from '../tree';
import { historyKeys, useHistory } from './api';
import { HistoryEntryItem } from './HistoryEntryItem';

/** Refetches the history when a change to this tree is saved (by this tab). */
function useRefreshOnSave(clanId: string) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  // Null until this tree is ready, so loading it does not count as a save.
  const version = useTreeStore((state) =>
    state.clanId === clanId && state.status === 'ready' ? state.confirmedVersion : null,
  );
  const seen = useRef<number | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const previous = seen.current;
    seen.current = version;
    if (previous !== null && version !== null && version > previous) {
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: historyKeys.list(clanId) });
    }
  }, [clanId, queryClient, version]);
}

export function HistoryPage() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const clan = useOutletContext<Clan>();
  const view = useClanTree(clan.id);
  const history = useHistory(clan.id);
  const tree = useTreeStore((state) => state.tree);
  const revertChange = useTreeStore((state) => state.revertChange);
  const lookup = useMemo(() => lookupIn(tree), [tree]);
  const [failure, setFailure] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const titleId = useId();
  useRefreshOnSave(clan.id);

  const canEdit = hasRoleAtLeast(clan.role, 'editor');
  const isOwner = clan.role === 'owner';
  const entries = history.data?.pages.flatMap((page) => page.entries) ?? [];

  const revert = (entry: HistoryEntry) => {
    const result = revertChange(entry.inverse, entry.seq);
    setFailure(
      result.ok
        ? null
        : t('history.revertFailed', {
            seq: entry.seq,
            reason: applyFailureMessage(t, result.reason),
          }),
    );
  };

  if (view.state === 'error' || history.isError) {
    const error = view.state === 'error' ? view.error : history.error;
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-start gap-3">
        <p role="alert" className="font-medium text-stamp">
          {t(errorMessageKey(error))}
        </p>
        <Button
          variant="secondary"
          onClick={() => (view.state === 'error' ? view.retry() : void history.refetch())}
        >
          {t('tree.retry')}
        </Button>
      </div>
    );
  }
  if (view.state === 'loading' || history.isPending) {
    return <p className="text-ink-muted">{t('common.loading')}</p>;
  }
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby={titleId} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <h2 id={titleId} className="text-xl font-semibold text-ink">
          {t('history.title')}
        </h2>
        <SaveStatus />
      </div>
      {failure !== null ? (
        <p role="alert" className="text-sm font-medium text-stamp">
          {failure}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      {entries.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="text-ink-muted">{t('history.empty')}</p>
      ) : (
        <ol aria-labelledby={titleId} className="flex flex-col">
          {entries.map((entry) => (
            <HistoryEntryItem key={entry.seq} entry={entry} lookup={lookup}>
              {canEdit && (isOwner || !needsOwner(entry.inverse)) ? (
                <Button
                  variant="secondary"
                  size="sm"
                  aria-label={t('history.revertLabel', { seq: entry.seq })}
                  onClick={() => revert(entry)}
                >
                  {t('history.revert')}
                </Button>
              ) : null}
            </HistoryEntryItem>
          ))}
        </ol>
      )}
      {history.hasNextPage ? (
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          className="self-start"
          disabled={history.isFetchingNextPage}
          onClick={() => void history.fetchNextPage()}
        >
          {t('history.more')}
        </Button>
      ) : null}
    </section>
  );
}
```

### Task 12: "Revert to this point" for the Owner

Spec §6.4: the Owner can go back to the state right after one change. The client fetches every change after it, flattens their inverses newest first into one batch (at most 500 steps; more gives "Choose a more recent point"), asks for confirmation with the count, and sends it as one op with `revertsToSeq`: all or nothing, one undo step. It is built from the log up to one version, so it is never rebased: the store refuses it (`OUT_OF_DATE`) while anything is pending or when the tree is at another version, and Task 6's rebase drops it when someone else saved first.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/features/history/revertPlan.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/features/history/RevertToPoint.tsx`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/features/history/HistoryPage.tsx`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/features/tree/store.ts` (`revertTo`, `ApplyFailure` gains `'OUT_OF_DATE'`)
- Modify: `apps/web/src/features/tree/format.ts` (`applyFailureMessage`)
- Modify: `apps/web/src/locales/vi.json`, `apps/web/src/locales/en.json` (`tree.outOfDate`, `history.revertTo.*`)
- Test: `apps/web/src/features/history/revertPlan.test.ts` (new), `apps/web/src/features/history/RevertToPoint.test.tsx` (new), `apps/web/src/features/tree/store.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `fetchHistoryPage(clanId, before, signal, limit)`, `historyKeys` (Task 11); `HistoryEntry`, `HISTORY_PAGE_MAX` (Task 5); `MAX_BATCH_OPS`, `SingleOp` (`@ancestors-tree/shared/tree`); `applyChange`, `toApplyResult`, `pushCapped` (Task 8); `needsRebase` (Task 6, store closure).
- Produces:
  - Store: `revertTo(op: Op, revertsToSeq: number, atVersion: number): ApplyResult`; `type ApplyFailure = OpErrorCode | 'NOT_LOADED' | 'OUT_OF_DATE'`.
  - `features/history/revertPlan.ts`: `revertStepsOf(entries: readonly HistoryEntry[]): SingleOp[]`, `type RevertPlan = { kind: 'ready'; op: Op; changes: number; atVersion: number } | { kind: 'tooMany' } | { kind: 'nothing' }`, `buildRevertPlan(entries: readonly HistoryEntry[]): RevertPlan` (entries newest first, all after the point), `fetchRevertPlan(clanId: string, seq: number, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<RevertPlan>`.
  - `RevertToPointButton({ clanId, entry })`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `apps/web/src/features/history/revertPlan.test.ts`:

```ts
import type { Op, SingleOp } from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { mockApi } from '../../test/api';
import { CLAN_ID, SON } from '../tree/fixtures';
import { FAMILY_HISTORY, historyEntry } from './fixtures';
import { buildRevertPlan, fetchRevertPlan, revertStepsOf } from './revertPlan';

const bio = (text: string): SingleOp => ({
  type: 'updatePerson',
  payload: { id: SON, changes: { bio: text } },
});
const batchOf = (ops: SingleOp[]): Op => ({ type: 'batch', payload: { ops } });

afterEach(() => {
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
});

describe('buildRevertPlan', () => {
  it('flattens the inverses newest first into one batch', () => {
    const entries = [
      historyEntry(5, bio('e'), bio('d')),
      historyEntry(4, batchOf([bio('b'), bio('c')]), batchOf([bio('c0'), bio('b0')])),
    ];
    expect(revertStepsOf(entries)).toEqual([bio('d'), bio('c0'), bio('b0')]);
    expect(buildRevertPlan(entries)).toEqual({
      kind: 'ready',
      op: batchOf([bio('d'), bio('c0'), bio('b0')]),
      changes: 2,
      atVersion: 5,
    });
  });

  it('sends a single step as itself, and has nothing to do without changes', () => {
    expect(buildRevertPlan([historyEntry(4, bio('b'), bio('a'))])).toMatchObject({
      kind: 'ready',
      op: bio('a'),
      changes: 1,
    });
    expect(buildRevertPlan([])).toEqual({ kind: 'nothing' });
  });

  it('refuses more than 500 steps', () => {
    const many = batchOf(Array.from({ length: 300 }, (_, i) => bio(String(i))));
    expect(buildRevertPlan([historyEntry(5, many, many), historyEntry(4, many, many)])).toEqual({
      kind: 'tooMany',
    });
  });
});

describe('fetchRevertPlan', () => {
  it('reads the pages back to the point, a hundred at a time', async () => {
    const [third, second, first] = FAMILY_HISTORY;
    const api = mockApi({
      [`GET /clans/${CLAN_ID}/history`]: ({ url }) =>
        url.searchParams.get('before') === '3'
          ? { body: { entries: [second, first], nextBefore: null } }
          : { body: { entries: [third], nextBefore: 3 } },
    });
    expect(await fetchRevertPlan(CLAN_ID, 1)).toEqual({
      kind: 'ready',
      op: batchOf([third!.inverse as SingleOp, second!.inverse as SingleOp]),
      changes: 2,
      atVersion: 3,
    });
    expect(api.calls.map((call) => call.path)).toHaveLength(2);
    const urls = vi.mocked(fetch).mock.calls.map(([input]) => String(input));
    expect(urls.every((url) => url.includes('limit=100'))).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

Create `apps/web/src/features/history/RevertToPoint.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { Op, SingleOp } from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import { screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { mockApi, type ApiCall } from '../../test/api';
import { renderRoute } from '../../test/render';
import { resetTreeStore, signOutTreeStore, useTreeStore } from '../tree';
import { CLAN, CLAN_ID, FAMILY, ME, SON, treeRoutes } from '../tree/fixtures';
import { FAMILY_HISTORY, historyEntry, historyRoutes } from './fixtures';

function ownerApp(options: { history?: typeof FAMILY_HISTORY; versions?: number[] } = {}) {
  const versions = options.versions ?? [FAMILY.version];
  let reads = 0;
  let seq = FAMILY.version;
  const posted: ApiCall[] = [];
  mockApi({
    ...treeRoutes(FAMILY, CLAN),
    // Each read of the tree may see a newer version (someone else saved).
    [`GET /clans/${CLAN_ID}/tree`]: () => {
      const version = versions[Math.min(reads, versions.length - 1)]!;
      reads += 1;
      seq = version;
      return { body: { ...FAMILY, version } };
    },
    ...historyRoutes(options.history),
    [`POST /clans/${CLAN_ID}/ops`]: (call) => {
      posted.push(call);
      seq += 1;
      return { body: { seq } };
    },
  });
  renderRoute(`/clans/${CLAN_ID}/history`);
  return { posted };
}

beforeEach(() => {
  signOutTreeStore();
  useTreeStore.getState().setAccount(ME.id);
});

afterEach(() => {
  resetTreeStore();
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
});

describe('Revert to this point', () => {
  it('is offered to the Owner on every change but the newest', async () => {
    ownerApp();
    await screen.findByRole('list', { name: 'Lịch sử thay đổi' });
    expect(
      screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Quay về thời điểm này: sau thay đổi #3' }),
    ).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Quay về thời điểm này: sau thay đổi #2' })).toBeEnabled();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Quay về thời điểm này: sau thay đổi #1' })).toBeEnabled();
  });

  it('is not offered to an editor', async () => {
    mockApi({ ...treeRoutes(FAMILY, { ...CLAN, role: 'editor' }), ...historyRoutes() });
    renderRoute(`/clans/${CLAN_ID}/history`);
    await screen.findByRole('list', { name: 'Lịch sử thay đổi' });
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: /Quay về thời điểm này/ })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('asks with the count, sends one op with revertsToSeq, and is one undo step', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const { posted } = ownerApp();
    await screen.findByRole('list', { name: 'Lịch sử thay đổi' });
    const opener = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Quay về thời điểm này: sau thay đổi #2' });
    await user.click(opener);
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole('dialog', { name: 'Quay về sau thay đổi #2?' });
    expect(await within(dialog).findByText('Số thay đổi sẽ hoàn tác: 1.')).toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Quay về' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog')).not.toBeInTheDocument());
    expect(opener).toHaveFocus();
    expect(useTreeStore.getState().tree.persons[SON]?.fullName).toBe('Nguyễn Văn Năm');
    await waitFor(() => expect(posted).toHaveLength(1));
    expect(posted[0]!.body).toEqual({
      op: FAMILY_HISTORY[0]!.inverse,
      baseVersion: FAMILY.version,
      expectedUserId: ME.id,
      revertsToSeq: 2,
    });
    expect(useTreeStore.getState().undoStack).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('counts every change after the point', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    ownerApp();
    await screen.findByRole('list', { name: 'Lịch sử thay đổi' });
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Quay về thời điểm này: sau thay đổi #1' }));
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole('dialog');
    expect(await within(dialog).findByText('Số thay đổi sẽ hoàn tác: 2.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('asks for a more recent point past 500 steps', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const bio = (i: number): SingleOp => ({
      type: 'updatePerson',
      payload: { id: SON, changes: { bio: String(i) } },
    });
    const many: Op = { type: 'batch', payload: { ops: Array.from({ length: 300 }, (_, i) => bio(i)) } };
    ownerApp({
      history: [historyEntry(3, many, many), historyEntry(2, many, many), FAMILY_HISTORY[2]!],
    });
    await screen.findByRole('list', { name: 'Lịch sử thay đổi' });
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Quay về thời điểm này: sau thay đổi #1' }));
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole('dialog');
    expect(
      await within(dialog).findByText(
        'Có quá nhiều thay đổi sau mốc này để hoàn tác cùng lúc. Hãy chọn một mốc gần hơn.',
      ),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(dialog).queryByRole('button', { name: 'Quay về' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('refuses when someone saved a change after it was prepared', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    // The page reads version 3; the check before sending finds version 4.
    const { posted } = ownerApp({ versions: [3, 4] });
    await screen.findByRole('list', { name: 'Lịch sử thay đổi' });
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Quay về thời điểm này: sau thay đổi #2' }));
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole('dialog');
    await within(dialog).findByText('Số thay đổi sẽ hoàn tác: 1.');
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Quay về' }));
    expect(await within(dialog).findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent(
      'Cây gia phả vừa có thay đổi mới. Hãy xem lại rồi thử lại.',
    );
    expect(posted).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(useTreeStore.getState().tree.persons[SON]?.fullName).toBe('Nguyễn Văn Nam');
  });
});
```

```ts
    });
    await store().load(CLAN_ID);
    const back: Op = { type: 'updatePerson', payload: { id: SON, changes: { bio: 'Cũ' } } };
    expect(store().revertTo(back, 2, 2)).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: 'OUT_OF_DATE' });

    store().apply(addPerson(NEW_ID));
    expect(store().revertTo(back, 2, 3)).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: 'OUT_OF_DATE' });
    held.open();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(store().pending).toHaveLength(0));

    expect(store().revertTo(back, 2, 4)).toEqual({ ok: true });
    expect(store().pending[0]).toMatchObject({ op: back, revertsToSeq: 2 });
    expect(store().undoStack.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ op: back });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(store().pending).toHaveLength(0));
    expect(bodies[1]).toEqual({ op: back, baseVersion: 4, expectedUserId: ME.id, revertsToSeq: 2 });
  });
```

(`gate`, `addPerson` and `NEW_ID` are the helpers the Task 8 undo tests already use in this file.)

```ts
      const state = get();
      if (
        state.status === 'ready' &&
        (state.pending.length > 0 || needsRebase || state.confirmedVersion !== atVersion)
      ) {
        return { ok: false, reason: 'OUT_OF_DATE' };
      }
      return toApplyResult(
        applyChange(op, { revertsToSeq }, (entry, inverse, current) => ({

          redoStack: [],
```

```ts
/** Why apply() did nothing: the broken rule, the tree is not loaded, or it changed meanwhile. */
export function applyFailureMessage(t: TFunction, reason: ApplyFailure): string {
  if (reason === 'NOT_LOADED') return t('tree.form.notLoaded');
  if (reason === 'OUT_OF_DATE') return t('tree.outOfDate');
  return t(ruleKey(reason));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Build the plan and the dialog**

Create `apps/web/src/features/history/revertPlan.ts`:

```ts
import { HISTORY_PAGE_MAX, type HistoryEntry } from '@ancestors-tree/shared';
import { MAX_BATCH_OPS, type Op, type SingleOp } from '@ancestors-tree/shared/tree';
import { fetchHistoryPage } from './api';

export type RevertPlan =
  | { kind: 'ready'; op: Op; changes: number; atVersion: number }
  | { kind: 'tooMany' }
  | { kind: 'nothing' };

/** The inverses of `entries` (newest first), flattened: undoing them in this order goes back. */
export function revertStepsOf(entries: readonly HistoryEntry[]): SingleOp[] {
  return entries.flatMap((entry) =>
    entry.inverse.type === 'batch' ? entry.inverse.payload.ops : [entry.inverse],
  );
}

/** One op that undoes every entry (newest first, all after the point), at most 500 steps. */
export function buildRevertPlan(entries: readonly HistoryEntry[]): RevertPlan {
  const newest = entries[0];
  const steps = revertStepsOf(entries);
  if (newest === undefined || steps.length === 0) return { kind: 'nothing' };
  if (steps.length > MAX_BATCH_OPS) return { kind: 'tooMany' };
  const [only] = steps;
  const op: Op =
    steps.length === 1 && only !== undefined ? only : { type: 'batch', payload: { ops: steps } };
  return { kind: 'ready', op, changes: entries.length, atVersion: newest.seq };
}

/** Reads every change after `seq` (newest first) and plans the revert; stops past 500 steps. */
export async function fetchRevertPlan(
  clanId: string,
  seq: number,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<RevertPlan> {
  const entries: HistoryEntry[] = [];
  let steps = 0;
  let before: number | null = null;
  for (;;) {
    const page = await fetchHistoryPage(clanId, before, signal, HISTORY_PAGE_MAX);
    for (const entry of page.entries) {
      if (entry.seq <= seq) return buildRevertPlan(entries);
      entries.push(entry);
      steps += entry.inverse.type === 'batch' ? entry.inverse.payload.ops.length : 1;
    }
    if (steps > MAX_BATCH_OPS) return { kind: 'tooMany' };
    if (page.nextBefore === null) return buildRevertPlan(entries);
    before = page.nextBefore;
  }
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/features/history/RevertToPoint.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { HistoryEntry } from '@ancestors-tree/shared';
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { errorMessageKey } from '../../shared/lib/apiError';
import { Button, Dialog, DialogClose, DialogContent, DialogTrigger } from '../../shared/ui';
import { applyFailureMessage, useTreeStore } from '../tree';
import { historyKeys } from './api';
import { fetchRevertPlan, type RevertPlan } from './revertPlan';

interface RevertToPointProps {
  clanId: string;
  entry: HistoryEntry;
}

function RevertToPointDialog({ clanId, entry, onDone }: RevertToPointProps & { onDone: () => void }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const load = useTreeStore((state) => state.load);
  const revertTo = useTreeStore((state) => state.revertTo);
  const [failure, setFailure] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  // Under the history key, so a refresh of the history plans again.
  const plan = useQuery({
    queryKey: [...historyKeys.list(clanId), 'revertTo', entry.seq],
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => fetchRevertPlan(clanId, entry.seq, signal),
    staleTime: 0,
    gcTime: 0,
  });

  const confirm = async (ready: Extract<RevertPlan, { kind: 'ready' }>) => {
    setBusy(true);
    // Takes a version someone else saved meanwhile, so the check below sees it.
    await load(clanId);
    const result = revertTo(ready.op, entry.seq, ready.atVersion);
    setBusy(false);
    if (result.ok) {
      onDone();
      return;
    }
    setFailure(applyFailureMessage(t, result.reason));
    if (result.reason === 'OUT_OF_DATE') {
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: historyKeys.list(clanId) });
    }
  };

  let body: string;
  if (plan.isPending) body = t('common.loading');
  else if (plan.isError) body = t(errorMessageKey(plan.error));
  else if (plan.data.kind === 'tooMany') body = t('history.revertTo.tooMany');
  else if (plan.data.kind === 'nothing') body = t('history.revertTo.nothing');
  else body = t('history.revertTo.count', { count: plan.data.changes });
  const ready = plan.data?.kind === 'ready' ? plan.data : null;

  return (
    <DialogContent title={t('history.revertTo.title', { seq: entry.seq })}>
      <p className="text-ink">{body}</p>
      {ready !== null ? (
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('history.revertTo.oneStep')}</p>
      ) : null}
      {failure !== null ? (
        <p role="alert" className="mt-3 text-sm font-medium text-stamp">
          {failure}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <div className="mt-6 flex justify-end gap-2">
        <DialogClose asChild>
          <Button variant="ghost">{t('history.revertTo.cancel')}</Button>
        </DialogClose>
        {ready !== null ? (
          <Button variant="danger" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void confirm(ready)}>
            {t('history.revertTo.confirm')}
          </Button>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    </DialogContent>
  );
}

/** "Revert to this point" (Owner). Radix returns focus to this button when the dialog closes. */
export function RevertToPointButton({ clanId, entry }: RevertToPointProps) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
      <DialogTrigger asChild>
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="sm"
          aria-label={t('history.revertTo.label', { seq: entry.seq })}
        >
          {t('history.revertTo.button')}
        </Button>
      </DialogTrigger>
      {open ? (
        <RevertToPointDialog clanId={clanId} entry={entry} onDone={() => setOpen(false)} />
      ) : null}
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

### Task 13: End-to-end: rebase, undo after a reload, revert to a point

One Playwright file runs the P6a flows against the real API and the production build, on the desktop and mobile projects: two tabs of the same account edit at once and the second rebases instead of reloading; an undo is saved and survives a reload; the Manager goes back to a point through the dialog (with an axe scan while it is open), and one undo brings everything back.

**Files:**
- Create: `e2e/undo-sync.spec.ts`
- Modify: `e2e/support.ts` (`addPerson`, `openActions`, `signIn`, `createTree`, `serverCounts`)
- Modify: `e2e/tree.spec.ts` (imports the moved helpers)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: every earlier task, through the UI.
- Produces (`e2e/support.ts`): `addPerson(page, fullName, gender?)`, `openActions(page, name)`, `signIn(page, email)`, `createTree(page, name): Promise<string>` (returns the clan id), `serverCounts(page, clanId): Promise<{ status: number; persons?: number; unions?: number }>`, `expectNoSeriousA11yViolations(page)`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Move the helpers**

Append to `e2e/support.ts` (and add `import AxeBuilder from '@axe-core/playwright';` and change the first import to `import { expect, type Page } from '@playwright/test';`):

```ts
/** Fills the person form that is open and adds the person. */
export async function addPerson(page: Page, fullName: string, gender: 'Nam' | 'Nữ' = 'Nam') {
  const dialog = page.getByRole('dialog');
  await dialog.getByLabel('Họ và tên').fill(fullName);
  await dialog.getByRole('radio', { name: gender }).check();
  await dialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'Thêm vào cây' }).click();
  await expect(dialog).toBeHidden();
}

export async function openActions(page: Page, name: string) {
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: `Thao tác với ${name}` }).click();
  return page.getByRole('dialog', { name });
}

/** Signs in on the login page that is open. */
export async function signIn(page: Page, email: string): Promise<void> {
  await page.getByLabel('Email').fill(email);
  await page.getByLabel('Mật khẩu', { exact: true }).fill(PASSWORD);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Đăng nhập' }).click();
}

/** Creates a family tree from the empty list page; returns its id. */
export async function createTree(page: Page, name: string): Promise<string> {
  await page.getByLabel('Tên cây gia phả').fill(name);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Tạo cây gia phả' }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/clans\/[0-9a-f]{24}\/tree$/);
  const clanId = /\/clans\/([0-9a-f]{24})\//.exec(page.url())?.[1];
  expect(clanId).toBeDefined();
  return clanId ?? '';
}

/** What the server stores, read with the page's session cookies. */
export async function serverCounts(page: Page, clanId: string) {
  const response = await page.request.get(`/api/clans/${clanId}/tree`);
  if (!response.ok()) return { status: response.status() };
  const tree = (await response.json()) as { persons: unknown[]; unions: unknown[] };
  return { status: response.status(), persons: tree.persons.length, unions: tree.unions.length };
}

export async function expectNoSeriousA11yViolations(page: Page): Promise<void> {
  const results = await new AxeBuilder({ page })
    .withTags(['wcag2a', 'wcag2aa', 'wcag21a', 'wcag21aa', 'wcag22aa'])
    .analyze();
  const serious = results.violations
    .filter((violation) => violation.impact === 'serious' || violation.impact === 'critical')
    .map((violation) => `${violation.id}: ${violation.help} (${violation.nodes.length})`);
  expect(serious).toEqual([]);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the e2e test**

Create `e2e/undo-sync.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, test, type Page } from '@playwright/test';
import {
  addPerson,
  createTree,
  expectNoSeriousA11yViolations,
  openActions,
  register,
  serverCounts,
  signIn,
} from './support.ts';

/** A new account with a tree whose progenitor is Phạm Văn Cả (changes #1 and #2). */
async function treeWithProgenitor(page: Page, slug: string): Promise<{ email: string; clanId: string }> {
  await page.goto('/register');
  const email = await register(page, 'Minh', slug);
  const clanId = await createTree(page, 'Họ Phạm');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Thêm người đầu tiên' }).click();
  await addPerson(page, 'Phạm Văn Cả');
  await (await openActions(page, 'Phạm Văn Cả'))
    .getByRole('button', { name: 'Chọn làm thuỷ tổ' })
    .click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('figure', { name: 'Phạm Văn Cả, đời thứ 1' })).toBeVisible();
  return { email, clanId };
}

test('two tabs edit at once: the second one rebases and keeps its change', async ({
  page,
  browser,
}) => {
  const { email, clanId } = await treeWithProgenitor(page, 'rebase');
  await (await openActions(page, 'Phạm Văn Cả')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Thêm vợ/chồng' }).click();
  await addPerson(page, 'Nguyễn Thị Ba', 'Nữ');
  await expect.poll(() => serverCounts(page, clanId)).toEqual({ status: 200, persons: 2, unions: 1 });

  // The same account in a second browser: it opens the tree as it is now.
  const second = await browser.newContext();
  const other = await second.newPage();
  await other.goto(`/login?returnTo=${encodeURIComponent(`/clans/${clanId}/tree`)}`);
  await signIn(other, email);
  await expect(other.getByRole('figure', { name: 'Nguyễn Thị Ba, đời thứ 1' })).toBeVisible();

  // The first tab saves a child; the second does not know yet.
  await (await openActions(page, 'Phạm Văn Cả')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Thêm con' }).click();
  await addPerson(page, 'Phạm Văn Hai');
  await expect.poll(() => serverCounts(page, clanId)).toEqual({ status: 200, persons: 3, unions: 1 });

  // Its change is sent on the old version: 409, rebase on the new ops, sent again.
  await (await openActions(other, 'Nguyễn Thị Ba')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Thêm con' }).click();
  await addPerson(other, 'Phạm Văn Tư');
  await expect(other.getByRole('figure', { name: 'Phạm Văn Hai, đời thứ 2' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(other.getByRole('figure', { name: 'Phạm Văn Tư, đời thứ 2' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect.poll(() => serverCounts(page, clanId)).toEqual({ status: 200, persons: 4, unions: 1 });
  await expect(other.getByText('Đã lưu', { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  await second.close();
});

test('an undo is saved, and the Manager goes back to a point and undoes that', async ({ page }) => {
  const { clanId } = await treeWithProgenitor(page, 'undo');
  // #3: a child (with a one-parent union); #4: a spouse who joins that union.
  await (await openActions(page, 'Phạm Văn Cả')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Thêm con' }).click();
  await addPerson(page, 'Phạm Văn Hai');
  await (await openActions(page, 'Phạm Văn Cả')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Thêm vợ/chồng' }).click();
  await addPerson(page, 'Nguyễn Thị Ba', 'Nữ');
  await expect(page.getByText('Hoàn tác: thêm Nguyễn Thị Ba')).toBeVisible();

  // #5: the undo.
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Hoàn tác', exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('figure', { name: /Nguyễn Thị Ba/ })).toHaveCount(0);
  await expect.poll(() => serverCounts(page, clanId)).toEqual({ status: 200, persons: 2, unions: 1 });

  await page.reload();
  await expect(page.getByRole('figure', { name: 'Phạm Văn Hai, đời thứ 2' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('figure', { name: /Nguyễn Thị Ba/ })).toHaveCount(0);
  // The undo steps belong to the page session: none after a reload.
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Hoàn tác', exact: true })).toBeDisabled();

  await page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Lịch sử' }).click();
  const history = page.getByRole('list', { name: 'Lịch sử thay đổi' });
  await expect(history.getByRole('listitem')).toHaveCount(5);
  await expect(history.getByRole('listitem').first()).toContainText('Hoàn tác thay đổi #4');
  await expect(history.getByRole('listitem').last()).toContainText('Minh: thêm Phạm Văn Cả');

  // Back to right after the progenitor was chosen: #3, #4 and #5 are undone at once.
  const opener = page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Quay về thời điểm này: sau thay đổi #2' });
  await opener.click();
  const dialog = page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Quay về sau thay đổi #2?' });
  await expect(dialog.getByText('Số thay đổi sẽ hoàn tác: 3.')).toBeVisible();
  await expectNoSeriousA11yViolations(page);
  await dialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'Quay về' }).click();
  await expect(dialog).toBeHidden();
  await expect(opener).toBeFocused();
  await expect.poll(() => serverCounts(page, clanId)).toEqual({ status: 200, persons: 1, unions: 0 });
  await expect(history.getByRole('listitem').first()).toContainText('Quay về sau thay đổi #2');

  // One undo brings everything back.
  await page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Cây', exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('figure', { name: /Phạm Văn Hai/ })).toHaveCount(0);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Hoàn tác', exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('figure', { name: 'Phạm Văn Hai, đời thứ 2' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect.poll(() => serverCounts(page, clanId)).toEqual({ status: 200, persons: 2, unions: 1 });
});
```

`openActions(…)` is awaited before `.getByRole(…)` because the dialog locator comes back from an async helper; keep the parentheses exactly as written (Prettier may wrap them as in `tree.spec.ts`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the ADR**

Create `docs/adr/0008-undo-rebase-and-revert.md`:

```markdown
# ADR 0008: Undo as new operations, rebase on conflict, and reverts from the history

- Status: Accepted
- Date: 2026-11-14

## Context

ADR 0007 gave every change an inverse and an append-only log, but on a 409 the browser reloaded the whole tree and dropped every unsent edit, an expired session followed by a Google sign-in lost the in-memory queue, and nothing used the inverses yet. P6a adds undo and redo, a history page with reverts, and sync that keeps edits when two relatives save at the same time.

## Decision

**The queue holds entries.** `pending` is a list of `PendingEntry { id, op, reverts?, revertsToSeq? }`. The local `id` ties an entry to its undo and redo steps and to an undo of it queued behind it. `reverts` names the change being undone: a seq once it is saved, otherwise the pending id, turned into the request's `revertsSeq` when the original gets its seq. Pure helpers (`features/tree/pending.ts`) replay entries on a tree and report what was kept, what was dropped and why, and the inverse each kept entry has on the new tree.

**Undo is a new operation.** The undo stack holds `{ op, inverse, ref }` for this user's changes in this tab, at most 100 steps, cleared on reload. Undo applies the inverse locally and queues it with `reverts`; it is never sent when it no longer applies ("Could not undo: <reason>", and the step leaves the stack). Redo applies the undone change again as a normal change, without `revertsSeq`. Every place where the queue changes also updates both stacks: a save names the step by its seq, a dropped entry leaves both stacks, a kept entry gets the inverse it has on the new tree.

**Rebase instead of reload.** On `VERSION_CONFLICT` the store reads `GET /ops?since=` page by page and rebases: the missed ops go onto the confirmed tree, the queue is replayed on top, and what no longer applies is dropped with the `rejected` notice. Two cases are special. When the missed ops contain exactly the head of the queue from this account, its answer was lost: it is confirmed with that seq, not replayed. A "Revert to this point" is dropped when someone else's op arrives, because it was built from the log at one version. The third conflict in a row, a gap in the log, or an answer that cannot be read still reloads the whole tree.

**Reverts from the history.** `GET /api/clans/:id/history?before=&limit=` lists operations newest first with the author's name, the stored inverse and the revert fields (50 per page, at most 100). "Revert" applies one stored inverse through the store, with `revertsSeq`, so it is optimistic, rebased and undoable like any edit. "Revert to this point" (Manager) flattens the inverses of every later change, newest first, into one batch of at most 500 steps sent with `revertsToSeq`; the store refuses it (`OUT_OF_DATE`) while anything is pending or when the tree is at another version. The server checks the range of both fields inside the write transaction and requires the Manager for `revertsToSeq`.

**The queue knows its account.** The app shell tells the store the signed-in account. Every `POST /ops` carries `expectedUserId`; a middleware that runs before the clan-role check answers `IDENTITY_MISMATCH` (409), and the store stops and keeps the queue until it learns the account changed. The queue is mirrored to `sessionStorage` with the user, clan and confirmed version, restored for the same account (through the rebase when the tree moved on) and dropped with a notice for another account or unreadable data. The login page's Google warning is gone.

## Consequences

- Two relatives editing at once keep both changes unless they really conflict; a conflict is explained per change instead of reloading everything.
- The log stays complete: undo, redo and reverts are ordinary operations, and the history says which change a revert undid.
- Undo depends on the inverses being exact, so the property tests now generate restore ops and check the person/union id overlap; a person's unions are capped at 100 so the inverse of a delete always fits one op.
- The undo stack does not survive a reload (the queue does). A Manager whose revert-to was refused prepares it again from the refreshed history.
```

At the end of the "Unsent edits are never lost silently" list in `docs/adr/0007-operation-log-and-optimistic-edits.md`, after the paragraph that starts "A notice stays until the user dismisses it", add:

```markdown
P6a replaces the reload on 409 with a rebase and persists the queue in `sessionStorage`, so the Google sign-in warning is gone: see [ADR 0008](0008-undo-rebase-and-revert.md).
```

§6.1 limits. Replace:

with:

§6.1 request fields. Replace the three bullets under "**Request fields besides `op` and `baseVersion`** (P6a):" with:

```markdown
- `expectedUserId` (required): the account the client's queue belongs to. When it differs from the signed-in user, the server answers `IDENTITY_MISMATCH` (409) and applies nothing. The check runs before the clan-role check, so another account's queue never gets a 404 or 403 that would make the client drop it. This stops one tab from sending another account's edits after a sign-in in a second tab.
- `revertsSeq`: set by undo and by "Revert" on the history page. It names the seq of the change being undone, must be at most the clan's version, and is stored on the Operation so the history can say "undid #42". Redo does not set it: it is the undone change made again.
- `revertsToSeq`: set by "Revert to this point". It needs the Owner, must be below the clan's version, and is stored on the Operation. Sending both fields, or a value out of range, is `VALIDATION_ERROR` (400).
```

§6.1 business rules. Replace:

```markdown
Deleting a person also deletes Unions where they were the only partner.
```

with:

```markdown
Deleting a person also deletes Unions where they were the only partner. Its inverse, `restorePerson`, brings back the person and those Unions; a Union that survived with the other partner keeps any later `order` or `status` edit.
```

§6.3. Replace:

```markdown
Retry at most 3 times; after that, reload the whole tree and tell the user.
```

with:

```markdown
The first and second conflicts in a row rebase; the third reloads the whole tree and tells the user (see "When to reload").
```

Replace the "**When to reload.**" bullet with these three bullets:

```markdown
- **A lost answer.** If the ops read in step 1 contain, from this account, exactly the op at the head of the queue, that op was saved before its answer was lost. It is confirmed with that seq instead of being replayed. The same check runs when a saved queue is restored onto a newer tree (§7.3).
- **"Revert to this point" is never rebased.** It was built from the log at one version, so when someone else's op arrives in step 1 it is dropped with the `rejected` notice.
- **When to reload.** After 3 conflicts in a row, when the pages of step 1 do not reach the server's version (a gap in the log), or when a successful answer cannot be read, the client reloads the whole tree, drops the pending ops and tells the user (the P5 behaviour). If the reload fails it is retried with the same backoff as sending, not in a tight loop.
```

```markdown
After `IDENTITY_MISMATCH` the queue is kept until the tab learns which account is signed in: the same account sends it again, another account drops it with the `discarded` notice (§7.3).
```

```markdown
Trên điện thoại thu thành thanh dưới cùng: Hoàn tác/Làm lại luôn hiện, phần còn lại trong menu "thêm".
```

with:

```markdown
Trên điện thoại thu thành thanh dưới cùng: Hoàn tác/Làm lại luôn hiện, phần còn lại trong menu "thêm", khi thanh công cụ có thêm nút ngoài Hoàn tác, Làm lại và trạng thái lưu; trước đó (P6a) thanh công cụ nằm trên khung cây ở mọi cỡ màn hình, để không che các nút của khung cây.
```

§7.8. Replace:

```markdown
- Bàn phím: Tab qua các thẻ, Enter mở hồ sơ, phím mũi tên di chuyển giữa cha/con/anh chị em.
```

with:

```markdown
- Bàn phím: Tab qua các thẻ, Enter mở hồ sơ, phím mũi tên di chuyển giữa cha/con/anh chị em. Bản thân khung cây là một điểm dừng Tab trước các thẻ: phím mũi tên di chuyển khung nhìn, + và − phóng to thu nhỏ, 0 xem cả cây, và các nút thu phóng nằm sau các thẻ (P6a). Thẻ nào nhận focus khi đang ở ngoài màn hình thì được đưa vào tầm nhìn, người vừa thêm cũng vậy (xoá người thì giữ nguyên khung nhìn).
```

Run: `grep -c "TOO_MANY_UNIONS\|revertsToSeq\|IDENTITY_MISMATCH" docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-11-ancestors-tree-design.md docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-11-ancestors-tree-design.vi.md`
Expected: the same count for both files.

- [ ] **Step 6: Run the whole check**

Run: `pnpm check`
Expected: PASS (Prettier also checks Markdown).

Write `.superpowers/sdd/p6a-task-14-commit.txt`:

```text
docs: ADR 0008, roadmap and spec for undo, the rebase and reverts

ADR 0008 records undo as new operations, the rebase on 409 with the
lost-answer check, reverts from the history and the account-bound,
persisted queue. The roadmap ticks the P6a items and lists what is left
for P6b-d. The spec (EN and VI) now matches what P6a built.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
```

```bash
pnpm exec prettier --write docs/adr/0008-undo-rebase-and-revert.md docs/adr/0007-operation-log-and-optimistic-edits.md docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-12-roadmap.md docs/superpowers/specs
```

**Placeholder scan.** Every code step has the code; every run step has the command and the expected result. Two steps tell the implementer to match an existing local name instead of inventing one: Task 11's store test uses the `store()`, `treeRoutes`, `OPS` and `ME` names Task 8 already uses in `store.test.ts`, and Task 12's uses `gate`, `addPerson` and `NEW_ID` from the same file.

**Type consistency.** Names checked across tasks:
- `PendingEntry`, `OpRef`, `UndoEntry`, `RedoEntry`, `pushCapped`, `resolveStack`, `withoutRefsTo`, `withoutPendingRefs`, `withInverses` (Tasks 6 and 8) are used with the same shapes in Tasks 11 and 12.
- `applyChange(op, extra, stacks)` and `toApplyResult` (Task 8) are used by `revertChange` (Task 11) and `revertTo` (Task 12).
- `ApplyFailure` gains `'OUT_OF_DATE'` in Task 12, together with `applyFailureMessage`.
- `HistoryEntry`, `HistoryPage`, `HistoryPageSchema`, `HISTORY_PAGE_MAX` (Task 5) are used by Tasks 11 and 12.
- `fetchHistoryPage(clanId, before, signal, limit)` (Task 11) is called with `HISTORY_PAGE_MAX` by `fetchRevertPlan` (Task 12).
- `describeOp`, `describeAction`, `lookupIn`, `lookupWith` (Task 8) are used by Tasks 9 and 11 (through the tree feature's `index.ts`).
- `treeOpenedBy` moves to `treePaths.ts` in Task 11.
- `useClanTree` (Task 11) replaces the load logic in `TreePage`; `TreeToolbar` (Task 9) keeps its place.
